{D845-1}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_/bi ma la pra b+hA nA ma mU la tan+t+ra nu sA ri NI dwA da sha sA ha sri kA la g+hu kA la tsakra tan+t+ra rA dza TI kA/_/bod skad du/_bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po 
[\[1b.2\]]dus kyi 'khor lo'i 'grel bshad/_rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rjes su 'jug pa stong phrag bcu gnyis pa dri ma med pa'i 'od ces bya ba/_dpal dus kyi 'khor lo la phyag 'tshal lo/_/stong nyid 
[\[1b.3\]]snying rje'i bdag nyid can/_/srid gsum skye dang 'jig pa med/_/shes dang shes bya gcig pa'i sku/_/dpal ldan dus 'khor la phyag 'tshal/_/rnam par bcas kyang rnam 
[\[1b.4\]]med bcom ldan 'das ma shes rab ma ste de yis 'khyud/_/skye 'jig spangs pa'i 'gyur med bde ba dang ldan bzhad pa la sogs bde ba spangs/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi skyed mdzad 
[\[1b.5\]]sku gsum dag dang ldan zhing dus gsum yang dag rig_/kun mkhyen mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan gnyis med de nyid la phyag 'tshal/_/sangs rgyas seng ge'i 
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]khrir bzhugs rdo rje'i rnal 'byor rnam par dag pa srid pa gsum gyis mchod/_/de nyid gcig pu sku yi rab tu dbye bas rnam pa bcu drug srid med srid par gnas/_/shes dang shes bya gcig gyur rgyal ba mchog gi dam tshig yan 
[\[2a.2\]]lag rnam pa bcu gnyis kyi/_/bden don byang chub sems ni rgyal ba'i rdo rje'i tshig gyur dus kyi 'khor lo la phyag 'tshal/_/kun mkhyen ye shes sku ni rgyal ba'i bdag po'i lhan cig skyes dang de bzhin chos kyi sku/_/longs spyod rdzogs dang sprul 
[\[2a.3\]]pa'i sku yang nyin byed sku dang pad+ma'i 'dab ma rgyas pa'i spyan/_/rnam par thar dang sku gsung thugs chags srid 'das 'byor pa rnal 'byor dag pa shes rab dang /_/thabs ni gnyis med gang zhig lha mi lha min gyis bstod dang po'i sangs rgyas de la 
[\[2a.4\]]'dud/_/ye shes stong nyid ye shes kyis/_/legs dag rnam dag 'gyur ba med/_/sems ni mtshan med ye shes kyis/_/legs dag gnyis med chos bdag nyid/_/ngag ni smon med ye shes kyis/_/dag pa gsang sngags mi zad sgra/_/
[\[2a.5\]]de bzhin mngon par 'dus ma byas/_/ye shes kyis dag dri ma med/_/shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid sbyor/_/dbyibs sku rdzu 'phrul bgrod pa can/_/de bzhin gshegs pas rdo rje sems/_/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che/_/dam tshig 
[\[2a.6\]]sems dpar gsungs pa gang /_/dngos dang dngos med zad pa'i gtso/_/thog mtha' med cing zhi ba yi/_/byang chub sems de la phyag 'tshal/_/sprul pa yi ni sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa dang /_/longs spyod rdzogs 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]pa'i sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa dang /_/dpal ldan chos kyi sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa dang /_/lhan cig skyes pa'i sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa ni/_/sad rmi gnyid stug bzhi pa yi/_/dbang po 
[\[2b.2\]]gnyis skyes pa de min zhing /_/ye shes sems dang ngag dang lus/_/gnas bzhir yang dag mi gnas te/_/las kyi phyag rgya yongs dor zhing /_/ye shes phyag rgya rnam par spangs/_/phyag rgya che las yang dag skyes/_/lhan skyes gzhan dang 'grogs pa min/_/rnam 
[\[2b.3\]]rtog bsgom pa las 'das pa/_/phyag rgya chen po mi 'gyur bde/_/gzung dang 'dzin pa dbyibs dag dang /_/rtog dang brjod pa rnam par spangs/_/dri za'i grong khyer rnam pa dang /_/pra phab pa yi rang bzhin can/_/thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid sbyor/_/e wa~M yi ge 
[\[2b.4\]]de la 'dud/_/phra rab rdul gyi chos nyid 'das/_/pra phab pa yi rang bzhin can/_/rnam pa kun gyi mchog ldan ma/_/phyag rgya chen mo de la 'dud/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi skyed mdzad ma/_/skye ba dang ni 'jig pa spangs/_/kun du bzang po'i spyod pa can/_/sna tshogs 
[\[2b.5\]]yum de la phyag 'tshal/_/A li kA li snyoms 'jug dang /_/hU~M phaT yi ge sogs spangs kyang /_/'gyur ba med las 'khrungs pa'i sku/_/dus kyi 'khor lo de la 'dud/_/kun mkhyen ye shes sku zhes pa/_/khyab bdag nyi ma lta bu'i sku/_/pad+ma'i 'dab ma 
[\[2b.6\]]rgyas pa'i spyan/_/de nyid bcu drug dbye ba las/_/sku bzhi'i bdag nyid sangs rgyas ni/_/rdo rje seng ge'i gdan bzhugs pa/_/lha dang lha min gyis bstod sngon/_/zla bzang gis gsol de la 'dud/_/ngag dang ye shes gcig sbyor rgyal ba mchog gi dam tshig 
[\[2b.7\]]thugs dang chos ni gcig tu sbyor/_/gsung dang longs spyod rdzogs pa gcig sbyor srid gsum gyis mchod sku dang sprul pa rnal 'byor te/_/shes dang shes bya gnyis med srid 'das 'byor pa 'di ni rdo rje sems dpa' rnam pa bzhi/_/rdo rje'i rnal 'byor thabs dang shes rab 
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]bdag nyid lha mi lha min gyis bstod de la 'dud/_/zhabs gcig rdo rje nor bur rdul dang nyi ma dang /_/gnyis pa gtsug tor khu ba ri bong can la ni/_/rtag tu mi chod dbyer med 'dod par bkod gyur pa/_/'jig rten gsum gyis mchod pa'i gtso la spyi 
[\[3a.2\]]bos 'dud/_/rlung me chu dang sa yi dkyil 'khor lhun po pad+ma dang /_/zla nyi dus me'i steng du dbang phyug dang ni bdud kyi mgor/_/zhabs ni srid pa'i yum gyi zhabs dang lhan cig bkod gyur pa/_/lha yi dbang pos btud pa'i gtso la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/_/de 
[\[3a.3\]]ltar rdo rje'i rnal 'byor gang zhig dpag med bde gshegs rnams kyis bsten cing bstan pa dang /_/byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje mchog 'dzin gsang sngags rig pa kun gyis yang dag brjod gyur pa'i/_/'gyur med mchog gi bde ba rnal 'byor de ni dus kyi 'khor lor 
[\[3a.4\]]blun po bdag brjod 'dod/_/mkha' la myur 'gro mkha' 'gro'i bdag po 'gro ste 'dab chags gzhan pa 'gro ba min nam ci/_gang du rang gi lus la srid pa mtha' dag sngags bcas pa/_/gsal bar A li thig le 'dzin pa rgyal ba'i dbang pos gsungs/_/chu 
[\[3a.5\]]gter chen po der ni rgyal ba'i slad du rab zhugs pa/_/de la gal te pha rol bdag phyin rgyal ba'i mthu yin no/_/gang phyir gsang bdag la gsungs mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las/_/stobs bcu ba yis bsdus pa'i rgyud ni sngon du lung bstan pa/_/de phyir dus 
[\[3a.6\]]kyi 'khor lo bsdus pa'i rgyud 'di ka lA par/_/'jam dpal gyis ni thub pa mtha' dag rnams la nges par bstan/_/'grel bshad stong phrag drug cu pa/_/zla ba bzang pos bris pa gang /_/theg pa kun don gsal byed de/_/pad+ma dkar po bdag gis ni/_/rtsa ba'i 
[\[3a.7\]]rgyud kyi rjes 'jug cing /_/gzhung ni stong phrag bcu gnyis te/_/rdo rje'i tshig 'byed dang bcas pa/_/bsdus pa'i rgyud la bri bar bya/_/rgyal bas gsungs pa'i cho gas dbang mchog blangs nas rgyud 'di'am ni bsdus pa yi/_/rgyud rgyal ma lus thos nas yang dag ye 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]shes lam la gnas pa'i rnal 'byor pas/_/khengs dang longs spyod 'byor pa du ma btang nas sems ni bla mar gtad byas te/_/sangs rgyas nyid kyi slad du gtso bo'i 'grel bshad gzhan phan spyod pa mnyan par bya/_/mngon shes med gang dpal ldan rdo rje theg pa la 
[\[3b.2\]]gnas bstan bcos du ma yi/_/rnam rtog chos kyi snying dang mkhas pa nyid kyi dregs ldan 'grel bshad chen po dag_/byed pa de ni glang chen long ba myos bzhin dgra yi rgyud ni rgyal ba'i slad/_/gang gnas de yis rang gi rgyud ni nyams byed 'di ni rtag tu bdud kyi gnyen/_/
[\[3b.3\]]mkhas pa'i mngon pa'i nga rgyal dang ldan rgyal bdag gsung ni mi shes ngang tshul rtag long bas/_/theg mchog 'grel bshad byas te long ba gzhan dag lhung du bcug nas dmyal bar rab 'jug pa/_/'gyod pa dang ni srog gcod la sogs mi dge'i lam la bud med bde bas 
[\[3b.4\]]sems drangs shing /_/dpal ldan shes rab bde nyams rdo rje'i tshig bcom phyi yi longs spyod dag gis zil mnan pa'o/_/gang zhig ston pa nyid kyis lung bstan rgyal ba dag bde ster rdo rje theg par mtha' dag ni/_/'grel bshad dag dang bsdu ba mdzad pa rdo rje mchog 'dzin 
[\[3b.5\]]bdag 'dra'i byang chub sems dpa' ste/_/de dag 'dir ni lha dang lag 'gro mi rnams smin pa'i don du sangs rgyas mthur smra la/_/gang dag mngon shes lnga med de dag bdag cag rgyal dbang yin zhes bden pa min pa smra/_/ma 'ongs dus na rtag tu rtog ge la sogs bstan 
[\[3b.6\]]bcos la gtad sna tshogs blos/_/sangs rgyas pa yang gzhan gyi rtog ge dang phrad dbang gis log pa 'byung 'gyur te/_/rtog ge la gtad de yis 'dod pa'i yul la lam ni ston par byed pa gang /_de dag nyid kyi blo ngan skyon ni 'joms byed 'grel bshad bdag gis bri bar bya/_
[\[3b.7\]]chags bral med pa'i 'dod chags mtha' yas chu la zhe sdang sbrul dang chu srin dkrigs/_/gti mug klong ni rab mang 'gro 'ong nor dang dpal la brkam pa'i rba rlabs can/_/'gyod sogs gsod pa la sogs las kyi rta gdong 'khor mo sgra drag srid pa yi/_/chu gter 
[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]la ni dpal ldan rdo rje theg pa'i 'grel bshad rtag tu mnyan pa ste/_shes rab rlung gi dar gyis myur 'gro myang 'das pha rol don gnyer chen po rnams/_/dam pa'i bde bas myur du rgal bar byed slad de ni lam gyi 'dren ma dang /_/nyon mongs bdud tshogs 
[\[4a.2\]]'joms pa mkhas pa'i yang dag ye shes yid bzhin nor bu ste/_/'jig rten gsum gyi bdag po nyid ster 'di ni kun du ston par byed pa'o/_/gang zhig phung po la sogs sgrib pa rab bcom yul ni pra phab brgyad lta bu/_/kun mkhyen bde ster de nyid gcig pu rdo rje 'dzin 
[\[4a.3\]]pa'i phyag rgya chen mo ste/_/de bor sangs rgyas nyid slad nor la chags shing 'khyog po'i snying ldan brtags ma dag_/gang gis sten byed rnam brtags blo can de yis de yang 'pho ba thob par byed/_/'dod chags nyams las sdig ste gang phyir shin tu sdug ma las ni zhe 
[\[4a.4\]]sdang skye/_/sdang las gti mug 'di las rang gi rdo rje lhung las sems ni rtag rmongs te/_/mchog min ma lus bza' dang btung ba la sogs yul gzhan dag la rab tu 'jug_/de yis sems la co dris bde ba nyams shing skye ba drug tu 'khor bar 'gyur/_/kun mkhyen 
[\[4a.5\]]lam don gnyer ba rnams la sgra dang sgra min rnam dpyad chen po med/_/sna tshogs yul skad ngan pa yis kyang chen po rnams kyi lam la rtag tu 'jug_/sems can rnams kyi mos pa'i sems kyi dbang gis thams cad mkhyen pa'i gsung gzhan la/_/lung ston dag la lha 
[\[4a.6\]]klus bkod pa'i sgra sogs rtsod pa don gnyer rnams kyi gzhan/_/yul gyi skad dang zur chag sgra las kyang /_/rnal 'byor ldan pas don ni 'dzin byed de/_/chu la 'o ma nges par zhugs pa de/_/ngang pas rab tu phyung nas 'thung bar byed/_/don dam de nyid yul la rtag 
[\[4a.7\]]tu ni/_/chen po rnam dag tshig la rton pa min/_/yul gyi ming rnams kyis ni don shes la/_/bstan bcos sgra dag gis ni ci zhig bya/_/gang zhig zur chag sgra dang sgrar gyur pas/_/brjod pa de nyid ye shes ma yin zhing /_/gang zhig 'gro ba dag la nyi tshe ba/_/de 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i gsung ma yin/_/mchog tu mi 'gyur bzhi pa de nyid ni/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi shes rab ye shes te/_/gang zhig de ni slar yang de bzhin 'di/_/rang gi phyag rgya chen mor rgyal bas gsungs/_/shes rab 'grogs las gang gi dkar po 
[\[4b.2\]]zag_/de yi bde ba ci yis 'phel bar 'gyur/_/dpyid dang 'grogs la me tog ma rgyas pa/_/ltung ba tsu ta'i 'bras bu ci yis 'byung /_/gang gis 'gyur med ma thob sdug bsngal can/_/de ni 'gyur ba dang bcas bde ba tshol/_/thams cad chu ni tshol byed tsA ta ka_/
[\[4b.3\]]skom par gyur kyang sa la gnas pa min/_/'khor ba yi ni bde ba mi rtag pa/_/'thob pa min yang rmongs chen tshol bar byed/_/yang dag rgyal srid bde ba thob pa yang /_/thar pa'i don du mkhas pas 'dor bar byed/_/ji ltar lo yi mtshams su rgya lam na/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]
[\[4b.4\]]zas can 'khrig pa'i bde ba tshol/_/rdo yi phye ma dag gi kha zas can/_/phug ron rnams ni rtag tu byed pa ste/_/lan cig rtag tu khu ba ltung ba'i phyir/_/gnyis ka la yang mchog gi bde ba med/_/de bzhin dka' thub can dang 'dod ldan la/_/rmi lam sad pa dag las 
[\[4b.5\]]'dzag phyir ro/_/ji ltar gnyid log la yang sbrul gyi ni/_/sos btab mi 'tsho de bzhin dka' thub pa/_/khu bsdud pa med phyir stobs med ma'i/_/skye gnas de yis nyams par byas pa'o/_/ji ltar rdo dreg skud pa'i gos dag ni/_/me yis bsregs pa nyid kyis dag 
[\[4b.6\]]'gyur ba/_/de bzhin skyes bu rnams kyi dam pa yi/_/sems ni shes rab ye shes dag gis so/_/ji ltar dngul chu me yis reg pa las/_/'bros par byed kyi g.yo ba med mi 'gyur/_/de bzhin thabs dang bral ba'i khu ba ni/_/rtag tu skye gnas reg pa dag las so/_/
[\[4b.7\]]ji ltar g.yo ba de yang sman sogs kyi/_/stobs kyis me dang phrad pas yang dag 'ching /_/de bzhin khu ba shin tu rnal 'byor gyis/_/shes rab dag dang yang dag phrad pas so/_/ji ltar me ni shing la rtag gnas kyang /_/gcad dang dbye ba'i thabs kyis mi mthong ste/_/gtsub 
[\[5a\]]
[\[5a.1\]]shing gtsub stan lag pas bsrubs pa las/_/de la gnas pa dag kyang mthong 'gyur ba/_/de bzhin sems kyi 'od gsal rtog pa yi/_/sgom pa'i thabs rnams dag gis mi mthong ste/_/de nyid du 'di rkyang ma dag dang ni/_/ro ma gcig tu sbyor bas 
[\[5a.2\]]mthong bar 'gyur/_/sna tshogs rnam rtog la sogs lam gyis ni/_/de nyid de bzhin gshegs pas gang gsungs pa/_/bcu drug bzhi yi rab tu dbye ba can/_/lam dang bral bas 'thob pa ma yin no/_/bskyed pa'i rim par gsungs pa'i hU~M dang ni/_/phaT 
[\[5a.3\]]yig la sogs rtog pa dang bral ba/_/rdzogs pa'i rim pa'i rnal 'byor de nyid de/_/de nyid kyi ni sgrub thabs gzhan yod min/_/du ba la sogs mtshan ma dag dang ni/_/srog rtsol dag gis dbu mar 'bab pa dang /_/rig ma'i brtul zhugs dag gis rdo rje ni/_/ltung 
[\[5a.4\]]min de bzhin steng du khu ba ste/_/lam 'di dag gis sangs rgyas rnams kyi ni/_/bde ba mchog tu 'gyur ba med pa zhes/_/mngon par brjod pa'am pho nya mo kun gyi/_/lhan cig skyes pa rnal 'byor pas thob 'gyur/_/gzugs la sogs par rtog pa dkyil 'khor gyi/_/
[\[5a.5\]]'khor lo bsgom pa goms par gyur pas ni/_/grub pa 'jig rten pa dag 'grub 'gyur te/_/'dod pa'i dngos grub chen po ga la zhig_/gang zhig sems can rnams la brtse ba yis/_/gzhan don dge la rtag tu brtson pa yi/_/slob ma dag pa rnams la lam dag ni/_/
[\[5a.6\]]bla ma dam pa'i drin lan 'byung ba yin/_/lam bzang rin chen 'di ni bag med kyi/_/sems can gzhan pa dag la byin pa na/_/rang khyim dgon pa dag tu rab zhugs la/_/yul ngan chom rkun rnams kyis 'phrog par byed/_/the tshom 'gyod pa dag dang gnyid dang ni/_/le 
[\[5a.7\]]lo rgod pa'i chom rkun rnams kyis kyang /_/gnyen 'dun thibs po'i nang du zhugs pa yi/_/lam bzang rin chen dag ni 'phrog par byed/_/srog gcod ma byin gzhan gyi bud med dang /_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]dang tshig rtsub dag dang phra ma dang /_/'khyal par gyur pa'i tshig dang 
[\[5b\]]
[\[5b.1\]]brnab sems dang /_/gnod sems lta ba ngan pa'i chom rkun dang /_/yod pa'i sdig dang nye ba'i sdig pa dang /_/gsod 'khu dbang po mngon par zhen pa ste/_/chom rkun lnga pa lnga po 'di dag gis/_/rin po che ni rtag tu 'phrog par byed/_/shin tu bza' dang btung ba sna 
[\[5b.2\]]tshogs dang /_/longs spyod du ma'i chom rkun de dag gis/_/brtson 'grus ldan du zin kyang bag med cing /_/brgyal bar gyur pa nyid la 'phrog par byed/_/rang nyid kyis ni yongs su ma brtags par/_/rin thang chen por gyur pa yin ma yin/_/shes par bya phyir g.yon 
[\[5b.3\]]can gzhan la ston/_/de yi tshig gis rin chen 'dor bar byed/_/gang dag rin chen bzang po rig pa rnams/_/rin chen chen po yongs su rtog tu gzhug_/de dag rnams kyi rnam dag tshig gis ni/_/rang gi rin chen rnam par shes par bya/_/rdzogs sangs rgyas dang byang chub 
[\[5b.4\]]sems rnams dang /_/pha dang ma dang bu mo sring mo dang /_/bu dang spun zla 'dod pa'i chung ma yi/_/gzugs rnams dag gis bdud ni bgegs byed do/_/de phyir bla ma dam pas gnang ba yi/_/lam bzang rin chen dag ni slob ma yis/_/chom rkun rtsa lag yongs su btang 
[\[5b.5\]]byas te/_/'bad pas shin tu legs par sba bar bya/_/dpa' bo'i rim pa bdag byin brlab pa yi/_/rim pa dag kyang thar pa'i lam ma yin/_/shin tu rnam dag rim pa gcig pu ni/_/thar pa'i slad du sangs rgyas rnams kyis bstan/_/mtshams med lnga po dag ni byas 
[\[5b.6\]]pa rnams/_/dbang don bde ba nyid ni gang zhig gis/_/thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid rgyud dag la/_/rgyal ba'i dbang pos yang dag gzung bar gyur/_/dpa' bo'i rim pa phyi rol la min te/_/lus la srog zad nyid la 'di gsungs so/_/bdag byin brlab pa dag 
[\[5b.7\]]ni stong pa la/_/khams gsum po ni mthong bar gyur pa'i mtshan/_/'khor ba snying po med de zad gyur pa/_/de las skyes bu'i sangs rgyas nyid 'bras te/_/chu shing gi ni 'bras bu smin pa dang /_/chu shing nyams pas yang dag 'byung ba bzhin/_/sdig pa dang ni shing 
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]srin sems can rnams/_/gang las byung ba de dag de nyams byed/_/de bzhin rnal 'byor dag gi sems de ni/_/sgyu ma rnam par nyams par byed pa yin/_/gnyis med g.yo ba med dang rigs med dang /_/'dod dang mchog tu mi 'gyur 'dod 
[\[6a.2\]]chags che/_/dngos dang dngos po med pa med pa dang /_/ye shes dang ni sems dpa' kun du bzang /_/nyon mongs med dang nyon mongs chen po dang /_/lhan skyes dpal ldan byang sems thig le 'dzin/_/dpal ldan dus kyi 'khor lo rdo rje dang /_/thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid rnal 
[\[6a.3\]]'byor ro/_/snying rje dang ni stong nyid sku/_/dus ni kun rdzob gzugs can ma/_/stong nyid 'khor lo zhes brjod de/_/dus kyi 'khor lo gnyis med 'dod/_/'di yi dpal ni myur thob slad/_/rtag tu rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs/_/rnal 'byor gang zhig sgom pa de'i/_/
[\[6a.4\]]bgegs ni rtag tu nyams par 'gyur/_/dgra dang seng ge glang chen dbang po me dang ltos 'gro'i bdag dang chom rkun zhags pas 'ching /_/chu gter 'khrug dang sha za 'chi ba'i 'jigs byed nad dang dbang po'i nye bar 'tshe ba dang /_/dbul dang bud med dang bral mi bdag 
[\[6a.5\]]'khrugs pa'i 'jigs dang rdo rje ltung dang nor nyams rnams/_/gang zhig rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs ni gsal bar dran byed de la rab tu nyams par 'gyur/_/glang chen shing rta rta dang rab tu mang ba'i mi mchog thams cad lus la go bgos shing /_/lag na mdung dang 'khor 
[\[6a.6\]]lo ral gri mda' gzhu'i dgra rnams kyis ni g.yul du 'joms byed pa/_/'chi ster drag po'i gzugs can phyogs dang mtshams son dgra de thams cad rang gi mtshon gyis ni/_/bcom ste rgyal ba'i grags pa gang zhig rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dran byed pas kun 
[\[6a.7\]]nas 'thob/_/kun nas seng ge'i sgra sgrogs shin tu rnam par 'gyur ba'i bzhin ldan mche ba rnon po gtsigs/_/rngam g.yo zhing sder mo gug la brtan pas glang chen myos pa'i klad pa rab tu 'gems/_/rab khros nyi ma 'char ka'i mig can ro 'dzin ral gri g.yo ba'i 
[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]gsod 'dod ral pa can/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de la ring du 'gro bar 'gyur/_/mdog sngo mig ni ser skya 'gram pa gnyis po myos pa'i chu bo rab tu mang bas gang /_/rnam mang ljon pa mchog rnams rtsad nas 'byin zhing rab tu 
[\[6b.2\]]grugs byed 'brug bzhin sgra dag sgrogs/_/lag pas bcing ba byas nas so yi rtse mo dag gis 'byed byed rab mchog glang po'i bdag_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de yi lus ni gtong bar byed/_/kun nas me lce rab 'bar phyogs 
[\[6b.3\]]dang mtshams su du ba kun gyis mun pa chen po gtibs/_/grong dang dgon pa dus gcig sreg cing skar mda' ltung ba bzhin du me stag 'phro bar byed/_/sems can rnams la dus kun du ni 'chi ba'i 'jigs byed phyogs kun khyab par gyur pa'i me/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma 
[\[6b.4\]]rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de la bsil ba nyid du 'gyur/_/khros shing mig sman sngon po'i mdog dang 'khyog por bgrod pa'i lus can drag po'i phaT sgra sgrogs/_/mche ba 'khyog po lce gnyis dang ldan 'chi ba'i 'jigs byed rlung gi shugs can rab tu gtum/_/drag

[\[7a\]]
[\[7a.1\]]bka' yis kun du srung ma rnams kyis srung byed pa/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de yang de las grol bar 'gyur/_/chu gter bzhon pa la zhon mtha' yas drag po'i rlung gis chu yi gter ni 'khrug byed cing /_/kun du 'khor

[\[7a.3\]]rung sgra sgrogs pa nas me 'bar lag na gri gug dang ni thod pa thogs/_/ro langs gdug pa'i sems ldan phyogs bcu'i dkyil 'khor dag la yang dag gnas shing 'jigs par byed/_/bkres pas gzir te sha dag za zhing mi khrag la dga' lus skams gos dang bral ba dag_/gang gis

[\[7a.7\]]par 'gyur/_/dbyar ni nags nas grong gi bar du 'dam rdzab kyis gang lam dag la yang khur khyer zhing /_/rgun ni gos dang bral bas kha ba'i lam du bgrod cing yul gzhan dag tu 'ang 'gro bar byed/_/so ga chu med 'jigs bcas mya ngan lam du nyi ma'i 'od zer gyis 
[\[7b\]]
[\[7b.1\]]gdungs dbul po gang /_/rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs bzang sgom byed sdug bsngal de dag gis btang 'dod dang ldan par 'gyur/_/mchan khung mig dang rna ba sna dang kha lus brjod pa dag las mi bzad dri 'byung zhing /_/bud med kun dang bral bas dus kun du ni dga' ba'i bde ba

[\[7b.3\]]'khrugs te 'jig pa'i gshin rje lta bur mtha' gcig tu/_/gsod pa la zhen 'chi ba'i 'jigs pa byed pa'i sde rnams myur du yang dag skul byed pa/_/rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs ni 'jig rten gsum po kun gyi mgon dag rang yid la bsgoms pas/_/de dag rnams kyi

[\[8a.1\]]'gyur med gcig 'di de dag de nyid gang gis dran/_/de yi skye ba 'di la ma lus 'jigs 'phrog 'dir brjod 'bras bu myur du 'byung bar 'gyur/_/dpal ldan rnal 'byor ma rnams dag gi gnas ni rdo rje nor bu'i khyim te der 'jug lam/_/nyon mongs

[\[9b.1\]]kyi tshogs dag dang skye ba snga ma'i stong pa nyid dang snying rje kun rdzob dang dmigs pa med pa'i bag chags kyi stobs kyis rdul phra rab 'dus pa las 'das shing /_dkyil 'khor la sogs pa rnam par rtog pa'i sgom pa dang bral ba phyag rgya chen po'i ye shes kyi dngos grub sgrub pa'o/_/de

[\[10a\]]
[\[10a.1\]]sogs pas nyin mo'i tshad la sogs pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_'jam dpal lung bstan pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_grub pa'i mtha' dang byed pa'i skar rtsis kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dbu ma'i rtsa'i dbugs rnams las 
[\[10a.2\]]gza'i rkang pa dang rgyu skar gyi rkang pa skye ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_khyim bcu gnyis kyi rnam pas sa 'khor ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dbyangs 'char ba la sogs pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_sgra gcan la sogs pa sa'i stobs kyi ngo bo nyid 
[\[10a.3\]]kyis gnas pa dang /_rdzong 'joms pa dang bsrung ba'i 'khrul 'khor gyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_'khor los sgyur bas dum bu bcu gnyis su 'khor ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_kla klo'i chos bcom nas sangs rgyas kyi chos rab tu 'jog pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis

[\[10b.4\]]gnas pa dang /_rtsa la sogs pa gza' yi rkang pa 'chad pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dbu mar zhugs pa'i dga' ba la sogs pa rnams kyis 'chi ltas kyi 'chi ba bslu ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_phyi rol 'jig rten pa'i bya ba bsgrub pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa 
[\[10b.5\]]dang /_lta ba thams cad rjes su dpag pas yongs su brtag pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_sems can sna tshogs kyi 'dod pa dang rjes su mthun par chos rab tu gzhag pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa zhes bya ba ni nang gi le'u la'o//_//de nas dbang gi 
[\[10b.6\]]le'u la dang por bla ma yongs su brtag pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_mchog dang 'bring dang tha ma'i slob ma yongs su brtags te bsdu ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_las rab 'byam bsgrub pa la grong la sogs pa'i phyogs kyi cha dang sa la sogs pa'i mtshan nyid 
[\[10b.7\]]kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_thab khung dang sbyin sreg gi rdzas dang bgrang phreng dang gdan dang phur bu dang bum pa dang rdul tshon dang thig skud dang 'khrul 'khor dgod pa'i mtshan nyid kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_sa yongs su gzung ba dang dkyil 'khor bri ba dang de nyid bcu 
[\[11a\]]
[\[11a.1\]]yongs su shes pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_'jig rten pa dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dbang sbyin pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_thams cad rab tu gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dam tshig gi 'khor lo 'dus 
[\[11a.2\]]pa dang dam tshig gi mchod pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_rigs sum cu rtsa drug gi lha mo'i phyag rgya dang lta stangs dang mtshan ma dang dam tshig gi brda dang /_rigs la sogs pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo gshegs su gsol ba'i ngo 
[\[11a.3\]]bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_chu bo chen po la sogs pa dang rang rang gi khyim du rdul tshon dang tshogs kyi 'khor lo gshegs su gsol te mchod pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dpal ldan bla ma dbul ba thams cad kyis mnyes pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa zhes bya ba ni dbang gi 
[\[11a.4\]]le'u la'o//_//de nas sgrub thabs kyi le'u la dang por bsrung ba'i 'khor lo la sogs pa dang gdong dag pa dang rang gi snying kha'i zla ba'i sa bon gyi 'od zer rnams las de bzhin gshegs pa spro ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_bla na med pa'i mchod pa dang 
[\[11a.5\]]sdig pa bshags pa dang bsod nams la rjes su yi rang ba dang /_skyabs su 'gro ba gsum dang bdag nyid dbul ba dang /_byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dang lam brten pa dang stong pa nyid la dmigs pa la sogs pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_skyed pa'i rim pa 
[\[11a.6\]]la dkyil 'khor rgyal mchog dang las rgyal mchog dang thig le'i rnal 'byor dang phra mo'i rnal 'byor la sogs pa'i sgrub thabs kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_phyi rol 'jig rten pa'i dngos grub kyi sgrub thabs kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_mchod sbyin la sogs pa

[\[11b\]]
[\[11b.1\]]bsgom pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa zhes bya ba ni sgrub thabs kyi le'u la'o//_//de nas ye shes kyi le'u la dang por lus kyi rus pa la sogs pa'i khams rnam par dag pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_sku dang gsung dang thugs dang rigs 
[\[11b.2\]]drug gi lha'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_a la sogs pa dang /_ka la sogs pa sngags kyi rigs drug gi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_thabs dang shes rab kyi rgyud sum cu rtsa drug gi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_thabs dang shes rab dang bya ba dang rnal 'byor rjes

[\[11b.4\]]gnas pa dang /_byang chub kyi sems bsten pa'i sgrub thabs kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_shes pa dang shes bya rnam pa kun gyi bdag nyid phyag rgya chen po'i dngos grub kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_lus dang ngag dang sems kyi bya ba thams cad rtag pa'i ngo bo nyid

[\[12a\]]
[\[12a.1\]]dag par bsdu ba dang rtsis kyi ming bsdu ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_yi ge lnga dang yi ge drug gi stong pa chen po dang thig le stong pa la bstod pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa zhes bya ba ni ye shes kyi le'u la'o//_//des na rim pa 'dis gsungs pa'i

[\[12a.7\]]'dod pa'o/_/de bzhin du dbang po gnyis kyi bde ba chen po 'dod pa ni nges pa dang bral ba rnams kyi rang gi sems kyi bsam pa ste/_rang gi sems kyi bsam pa 'dis skye ba 'di la sangs rgyas nyid kyi 'bras bu rab tu ster ba zhes bya ba ni skye ba 'di mi'i skye ba la sangs rgyas nyid kyi 
[\[12b\]]
[\[12b.1\]]'bras bu rab tu ste rab rgyud kyi rgyal po'o/_/lha la sogs pa 'gro ba lnga'i skye ba la ni ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_las kyis ma yin par skyes pa'i phyir ro/_/'dir lha la sogs pa rnams la yang mi'i skye ba thob nas sangs rgyas nyid kyi 'bras bu ster bar 'gyur ro/_/zhes bya

[\[12b.3\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i nges pa'o/_/ci ste sngags kyi theg pa 'dir sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa ni 'di lta ste/_gdol pa smyig ma mkhan sogs dang /_/mtshams med lnga ni byed pa rnams/_/sngags kyi spyod pa rjes spyad na/_/skye 'di nyid la sangs

[\[13a\]]
[\[13a.1\]]zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa'i phyir sngags kyi theg pa la zhugs pa'i rjes la sdig pa byas kyang sangs rgyas nyid kyi 'bras bu thob par 'gyur ro zhes pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i nges pa ma yin no/_/'o na sngags kyi theg pa 'dir rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis sha

[\[15a\]]
[\[15a.1\]]khri gcig stong drug brgya rnams 'gog pa'o/_/de 'di la yod pa ni chos kyi sprin gyi sa ste de la gnas pa ni ro ldan na gnas pa'o/_/bdud dang nyon mongs pa dang shes bya dang snyoms 'jug gi sgrib pa'i gnod sbyin rnams gsod par byed pa ni gnod sbyin gyi bdag po

[\[15a.3\]]gi dang po'i sangs rgyas rjod par byed pa'i phyir te brjod bya 'di rjod par byed pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa'i phyir mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas so/_/dus kyi 'khor lo ni brjod bya dus kyi 'khor lo rjod par byed pa'i phyir dus kyi 'khor lo'i rjod byed do/_/stong phrag bcu gnyis

[\[15a.6\]]rab kyi bdag nyid can gyi rgyud kyi rgyal po 'di la/_thabs dang shes rab kyi phyogs tha dad na thabs dang shes rab kyi bdag nyid can gyi brjod par bya ba med par 'gyur ro/_/thabs dang shes rab kyi bdag nyid can gyi brjod par bya ba med na gnyis su med pa'i ye shes med 
[\[15a.7\]]par 'gyur ro/_/gnyis su med pa'i ye shes med na sangs rgyas nyid kyang med par 'gyur ro/_/sangs rgyas nyid med na 'khor ba'o/_/'dir rjod par byed pa dang brjod par bya ba 'brel pa med par thabs kyi ming can gyi rnal 'byor gyi rgyud la thabs dang shes rab 
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]kyi bdag nyid gnyis su med pa dang rnal 'byor brjod bya rjod byed kyi mtshan nyid du mi 'gyur ro/_/rnal 'byor gyi sgra ni thabs kyi don rjod par byed pa'am shes rab kyi don rjod par byed pa yang ma yin te rnal 'byor gyi sgra ni thabs dang shes rab kyi don rjod par byed pa'o/_/de bzhin du yang

[\[15b.3\]]yod pa'i rgyud de ni don dam par shes rab kyi rgyud kyang ma yin la thabs kyi rgyud kyang ma yin no/_/'jig rten gyi kun rdzob tu me long lta bu'i ye shes la sogs pa'i dbye bas phung po dang khams rnam par dag pa las thabs dang shes rab kyi phyogs su dbang po rtul po'i sems can 
[\[15b.4\]]gyi bsam pa'i dbang gis de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs so/_/de bzhin du rnal 'byor ma'i ming can la yang ngo /_/'dir 'di la rnal 'byor gyi ngang tshul yod pa'i phyir rnal 'byor pa'o/_/de bzhin du rnal 'byor ma yang ngo /_/de'i phyir gzhan la ltos pa nyid 
[\[15b.5\]]las rnal 'byor gyi rgyud dang rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud du yang 'gyur ro/_/des na don dam pa'i bden pas thabs dang shes rab kyi bdag nyid can rnal 'byor gyi rgyud rigs med pa dus kyi 'khor lo'o/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa'i ye shes kyi sku mngon du gyur pa'i mtshan yang dag par brjod

[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]gsungs pa ni ma yin no/_/'dir phyag na rdo rjes/_'das pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis gsungs/_/ma 'ongs rnams kyang gsung 'gyur la/_/da ltar byung ba rdzogs sangs rgyas/_/yang yang gsung ba gang yin dang /_/rgyud chen sgyu 'phrul dra ba

[\[16a.7\]]kyi rdo rje'i tshig mngon du gyur pa zhes pa ni rdo rje theg pa 'dir sems can gyi bsam pa'i dbang gis rgyud kyi rgyal po thams cad la rdo rje'i tshig tu sbas te bzhi pa de yang de bzhin no zhes de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/bzhi pa ni gsum pa ma yin te bzhi pa

[\[20a.7\]]'khyil pa dang mngon par shes pa drug dang ldan pa ma yin no/_/de'i phyir bdud ces bya ba ni sems can rnams kyi 'khor ba'i sems kyi bag chags kyi dri ma'o/_/sangs rgyas nyid ces bya ba ni 'khor ba'i bag chags dang bral ba'i sems so/_/de ltar yang bcom ldan 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]'das kyis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las/_sems gang zhig yod pa'i sems de ni sems ma yin no zhes rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal bar gsungs te de nyid ni 'khor ba'i bag chags dang bral ba'o/_/de'i phyir bdud ni dri ma dang bcas pa'i sems yin la sangs rgyas nyid 
[\[20b.2\]]ni dri ma med pa'i sems yin no/_/'dir phyi rol du sangs rgyas kyis bdud bcom pa gang yin pa de ni rmi lam bzhin du sems can rnams kyi rang gi sems kyi snang ba yin no/_/don dam par de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs ni le'u lnga par rgyas par brjod par bya'o/_/'dir 
[\[20b.3\]]sems can gyi don du sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'khor bar 'jug pa ni 'khor ba pa rnams kyis brjod par bya ba ma yin no/_/'jig rten dbang phyug bdag sems can gyi don la dmyal bar zhugs pa'i dus su gshin rje'i pho nya rnams dang gshin rje'i 
[\[20b.4\]]rgyal po rnams kyis bstod pa brjod pa/_gang zhig srid pa'i 'ching ba rnams las grol yang sems can don du srid pa 'dzin/_/de ni stong pa'i don dag ston mdzad dus las las kyi 'bras bu gtong ma yin/_/sems kyi dri ma yang dag ye shes mes bsregs rtag tu 
[\[20b.5\]]yang dag brtse bas brlan/_/mdzad spyod brtag min sems can don la dgyes pa'i sangs rgyas de la bdag cag 'dud ces pa'o/_/de'i phyir sems can gyi don gyi ngo bor sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams mngal du 'jug pa'am dmyal bar gshegs pa ni byis 
[\[20b.6\]]pa'i skye bo rnams kyis rnam par brtag par mi nus so/_/pha rol tu phyin pa bcu bsgrubs pa sa bcu'i dbang phyug dbang bcu thob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' gcig gis kyang stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams su sprul pa'i sku du ma dag gis byang chub 
[\[20b.7\]]sems dpa'i gdul bya rnams la byang chub sems dpa'i chos ston par byed de byang chub sems dpa' chen po de du ma ni ma yin no/_/de bzhin du sngon gyi smon lam gyi stobs kyis bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs yongs su rdzogs shing mi 'jigs pa la sogs pa'i 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]dbang phyug gi yon tan thams cad phun sum tshogs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams rnams su sgyu ma'i sprul pa'i sku sgrib pa dang bral ba mtha' yas pa mtha' yas pa dag gis/_
[\[21a.2\]]sems can mtha' yas pa mtha' yas pa dag gi skad mtha' yas pa mtha' yas pa dag gis gsol ba 'debs pa po sna tshogs kyis gsol ba btab pa na/_sems can thams cad kyi skad la rab tu 'jug pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i skad kyis 'jig rten dang 'jig 
[\[21a.3\]]rten las 'das pa'i chos ston par mdzad de thams cad mkhyen pa de du ma ni ma yin no/_/dper na sgyu ma'i skyes bu chen po 'ga' zhig gis sgyu ma'i gzugs du ma sprul te des sprul pa'i gzugs de dag gis shing rnams rtsa ba nas 'byin par byed cing ri'i rtse mo yang skyod 
[\[21a.4\]]par byed la/_lha chen po'i gzugs lta bu dang khyab 'jug lta bus lha sbyin la sogs pa 'ching bar byed de sgyu ma'i skyes bu chen po de du ma ni ma yin no/_/de bzhin du sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sgyu ma'i sprul pa'i sku dag gis srid pa gsum du 
[\[21a.5\]]sems can gyi don mdzad pa'o/_/des na sems can thams cad la 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos bstan pa'i phyir sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das sngar sa bcu gnyis kyi dbang phyug tu gyur pa mkhas pa sgyu 'phrul chen po 'chang ba sgyu 'phrul chen po'i mig 'phrul 
[\[21a.6\]]dang ldan pa 'phags pa'i yul lum+bi nir shAkya'i rigs mi'i dbang po zas gtsang ma'i lha mo sgyu 'phrul chen mo'i lhums nas bltams pa ni gzhon nu don grub yin la/_sa bcu'i dbang phyug phyag na rdo rje yang sham+b+ha la'i yul ka lA pa'i grong du nyi ma'i 'od kyi lha mo rnam par rgyal 
[\[21a.7\]]ma'i mngal nas 'khrungs pa zla ba bzang po'o/_/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing du 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos bstan pa'i don dus bcu gnyis mngon du mdzad nas shAkya thub par gyur to/_/byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje ni 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]chu bo shI ta'i byang phyogs grong bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa'i gdul bya rnams rgyud kyi rgyal po mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas kyis yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi lam thob par bya ba'i phyir rgyal po zla ba bzang por gyur to/_/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' 'di 
[\[21b.2\]]gnyis sprul pa'i sku'am longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku dag gis ston pa po dang gsol ba 'debs pa por 'brel ba yin te snga ma dang phyi ma 'gal ba med pa'i phyir ro/_/phyag na rdo rje'i sprul pa'i sku rgyal po zla ba bzang po des sems can thams cad kyis 'jig rten dang 'jig rten 
[\[21b.3\]]las 'das pa'i dngos grub bsgrub pa'i don du de bzhin gshegs pa la gsol ba btab bo/_/de bzhin gshegs pas kyang chu bo shI ta'i byang phyogs sham+b+ha la la sogs pa'i yul rnams kyi grong bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa rnams kyi sems rnam par dag pa skal ba dang 
[\[21b.4\]]ldan pa nyid du gnas pa gzigs nas rdo rje'i tshig kha ma phye ba med pa rdo rje'i tshig rab tu gsal bar byed pa mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas stong phrag bcu gnyis pa yang dag par bstan to/_/ma 'ongs pa'i dus su tshangs pa'i drang srong bye ba phrag phyed dang bzhi dang /_grong bye ba 
[\[21b.5\]]phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa rnams yongs su smin par gzigs nas/_rgyud kyi rgyal po stong phrag bcu gnyis pa 'di las bsdus pa'i rgyud byed pa dang rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po ston par byed pa dang /_rdo rje'i rigs kyi dbang bskur bas rigs thams cad rigs gcig tu 
[\[21b.6\]]byed pa la 'jam dpal grags pa rigs ldan du de bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan to/_/'grel bshad byed pa po pad+ma dkar po bdag kyang rigs ldan gnyis par lung bstan to/_/de'i 'og tu bdag gi phyi nas gshin rje mthar byed la sogs pa rnams lung bstan to/_/'dir 
[\[21b.7\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis/_lo 'di nas ni drug brgya'i los/_/drang srong rnams ni smin don du/_/'jam dbyangs mi bdag grags pa ni/_/sham+b+ha la zhes bya bar 'byung /_/'di yi sgrol ma lha mo che/_/sras ni pad 'dzin 'jig rten dbang /_/bdag gi shAkya'i 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]rigs rgyud las/_/byung ba zla bzang khyod rigs la'o/_/smra mkhas rdo rje'i rigs dang ldan/_/grags pa rdo rje'i rigs la ni/_/rigs bzhi rigs gcig byas gang des/_/rigs ldan tshangs pa'i rigs kyis min/_/'di skad bdag gis thos 
[\[22a.2\]]pa 'dis/_/drang srong rnams la chos bstan na/_/gzhan las thos phyir kun mkhyen min/_/zhes pa'i rtsod pa 'byung bar 'gyur/_/rnam pa gang dang gang dag gis/_/sems can rnams ni yongs smin byed/_/rnam pa de dang de dag gis/_/chos ni bstan par bya 
[\[22a.3\]]ba yin/_/sgra dang sgra nyams dag gi chos/_/rnal 'byor bas ni 'bad pas 'dzin/_/yul gyi sgra yis don rnyed pa/_/de la bstan bcos sgra yis ci/_/ji ltar sa la rin chen ming /_/dbye ba tha dad tha dad ni/_/yul dang yul gyi dbang gis brjod/_/rin chen 
[\[22a.4\]]dbye ba 'gar yang med/_/de bzhin bdag gi dag pa'i chos/_/sdud par byed pa sna tshogs kyis/_/sems can bsam pa'i dbang gis ming /_/sna tshogs tha dad tha dad brjod/_/des na snying po'i don bsdus 'di/_/thams cad mkhyen sogs bdag 'dod do/_/
[\[22a.5\]]phreng 'dzin tshigs bcad sum cu ni/_/lhag pa'i phyogs brgya le'u lngas/_/dpal ldan rgyud dag rdzogs par ni/_/smra ba'i rgyal pos ston 'gyur te/_/sdud par byed pa po yang 'di/_/'grel bshad byed pa pad dkar 'dzin/_/rgyud 'dir drang srong rigs sogs la/_/
[\[22a.6\]]sangs rgyas lam ni gsal byed pa/_/zla ba lha dbang gzi brjid can/_/zla bas byin dang lha dbang phyug_/sna tshogs gzugs dang lha dbang ldan/_/grags pa pad dkar rim pas so/_/'das pa'i rgyal po nyi ma'i 'od/_/de ni bgegs dgra'i sprul pa ste/_/phyag na 
[\[22a.7\]]rdo rje zla bzang khyod/_/sa snying gshin rje mthar byed dang /_/sgrib pa thams cad rnam sel dang /_/rmugs byed khengs byed rim pa ste/_/nam mkha'i snying po 'jam dbyangs dang /_/'jig rten mgon po [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/gshin rje gshed sogs khro bo bcu/_/de bar 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]byang chub sems dpa' gzhan/_/bcu gsum de dag rim pa yis/_/rigs ldan rigs la 'byung bar 'gyur/_/grags pa rigs ldan rigs kyang ste/_/de nas rigs ldan pad+ma dkar/_/bzang po rigs ldan gsum pa ste/_/de bzhin bzhi pa rnam rgyal lo/_bshes 
[\[22b.2\]]gnyen bzang po phyag dmar dang /_/bdun pa khyab 'jug sbas pa'o/_/nyi ma grags dang shin tu bzang /_/rgya mtsho rnam rgyal rgyal dka' dang /_/rigs ldan nyi ma bcu gnyis pa/_/sna tshogs gzugs dang zla ba'i 'od/_/mtha' yas dang ni sa skyong dang /_/dpal skyong 
[\[22b.3\]]seng ge rnam par gnon/_/stobs po che dang ma 'gags pa/_/mi yi seng ge dbang phyug che/_/mtha' yas rnam rgyal rigs ldan dang /_/grags pa rigs ldan de nas slar/_/de sras 'khor lo chen po can/_/rigs ldan drag po 'byung 'gyur te/_/smra mkhas rta mchog 
[\[22b.4\]]ting 'dzin gyis/_/kla klo'i chos ni mthar byed pa'o/_/nyi ma'i shing rta la sogs kyi/_/smra mkhas ston pa 'byung 'gyur gang /_/de yis zla bzang rtsa rgyud la/_/khyod ni sdud par byed pa ste/_/'dir ni sems can yongs smin byed/_/khyod nyid 'grel bshad byed 
[\[22b.5\]]pa'o/_/'jam pa'i rdo rjes bsdus rgyud la/_/'grel bshad byed pa pad 'dzin rang zhes gsungs so/_/rtsa ba'i rgyud pas ji ltar gsungs pa'i rim pas byang chub sems dpa' dang khro bo'i rgyal po lung bstan pa rnams grong bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug gi 'khor los 
[\[22b.6\]]sgyur ba sngags kyi tshul ston par byed pa por de bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan to/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i nges pa de'i phyir rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po stong phrag bcu gnyis pa dang 'grel bshad stong phrag drug cu pa yang sham+b+ha la la sogs pa'i yul gyi skad gzhan gyis glegs 
[\[22b.7\]]bam la bris nas rgyal po zla ba bzang pos grong bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa rnams la rab tu bstan to/_/de dag nyid de la mos pa rnams kyis mnyan zhing bklags te rang gi sems la bzung nas gzhan dag la yang rgya cher rab tu bstan to/_/rgyud bstan pa'i dus de nas 
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]lo gnyis pa la sems can rnams kyi dngos grub kyi rgyu'i slad du dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo dang rdzu 'phrul bstan nas sprul pa'i skus gang nas byon pa de nyid du longs spyod rdzogs pa'i skus gshegs so/_/de nas lha'i dbang gis lo brgya'i bar du rgud ston 
[\[23a.2\]]pa mdzad de/_de bzhin du gzi brjid can dang /_zla bas byin dang /_lha'i dbang phyug dang /_sna tshogs gzugs dang /_lha'i dbang ldan gyis kyang ngo /_/nam mkha'i snying po'i sprul pa'i sku lha'i dbang ldan 'di'i lha mo sna tshogs yum gyi mngal du 'jam dpal rgyal 
[\[23a.3\]]po grags par gyur te byang chub sems dpa' seng ge'i khri de la lo brgya'i bar du chos bstan to/_/de nas lo brgya rdzogs pa'i rjes la de bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs kyis/_/drang srong rnams yongs su smin pa'i dus su gzigs shing mngon 
[\[23a.4\]]par shes pa lnga'i stobs kyis yang dag pa'i lam thob par mkhyen nas nges pa ster bar bzhed pa'i rgyal po grags pas thams cad bos par gyur to/_/ka lA pa'i grong gi lho phyogs na ma la ya'i skyed mos tshal te rgyar dpag tshad bcu gnyis pa ka lA pa'i grong dang mnyam pa'o/_/
[\[23a.5\]]de'i shar phyogs na nye ba'i yid kyi mtsho rgyar dpag tshad bcu gnyis pa'o/_/nub phyogs na pad+ma dkar po'i mtsho tshad de dang 'dra'o/_/mtsho de gnyis kyi bar na ma la ya'i skyed mos tshal lo/_/ma la ya'i skyed mos tshal gyi dbus na rgyal po zla ba bzang pos mdzad pa bcom 
[\[23a.6\]]ldan 'das dus kyi 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo ste rin po che lnga'i rang bzhin lha dang lha mo'i bdag nyid yongs su grub pa gru bzhi pa/_rgyar khru bzhi brgya yod pa/_phyi rol du sku'i dkyil 'khor gru bzhi pa/_sgo bzhi pa/_rta babs bzhi dang dur khrod brgyad kyis 
[\[23a.7\]]brgyan pa/_rab lngas bskor ba/_phyi rol dus la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor bzhi dang /_rdo rje phreng bas brgyan pa ste rdo rje phreng ba'i mthar thug pa'i rgyar ni khru brgyad brgya'o/_/sku'i dkyil 'khor gyi phyed kyi tshad dbus su gru bzhi pa ni gsung gi dkyil 'khor te gru bzhi pa/_sgo 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]bzhi pa/_rta babs bzhis brgyan pa/_ra ba lngas bskor ba'o/_/gsung gi dkyil 'khor gyi phyed kyi tshad ni thugs kyi dkyil 'khor te gru bzhi pa/_sgo bzhi pa/_rta babs bzhis brgyan pa/_ra ba gsum gyis bskor ba'o/_/de'i phyed kyis ye shes kyi 'khor lo ste/_ka ba 
[\[23b.2\]]bcu drug gis nye bar mdzes pa'o/_/de'i phyed kyis pad+ma 'dab ma brgyad pa ste pad+ma'i sum cha ni lte ba'o/_/de ltar sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi dkyil 'khor rnams mtshan nyid thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa do shal dang do shal phyed pa dang yang dag par ldan pa/_rin po 
[\[23b.3\]]che'i snam bu dang /_kha khyer dang /_ba ku lI dang bcas pa/_me long dang zla ba phyed pa dang dril bus rnam par mdzes pa'o/_/dkyil 'khor gsum gyi bdag nyid kyi dkyil 'khor gyi khang pa der dbo'i zla ba'i nya la nyi ma'i shing rta la sogs pa tshangs pa'i drang srong bye ba phrag 
[\[23b.4\]]phyed dang bzhi la rgyal po grags pas nges pa byin pa ni nyi ma'i shing rta la sogs pa tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams bdag gi tshig thams cad mkhyen pa'i phun sum tshogs pa byed pa nyon cig_/nag pa'i zla ba'i nya 'di la rig byed kyi gzhung gi nges pa skyong bar byed pa khyed rnams la 
[\[23b.5\]]bdag gis bstan pa sbyin par bya'o/_/de bas na khyed kyis yul sna tshogs pa gzhan gyi rigs dang ldan pa'i bram ze gang yin pa de dag tha dad du byas te bdag la bstan par bya'o/_/tshig des yul sna tshogs kyi spyod pa thod par za ba dang ba lang gi sha dang ma he'i sha za ba dang 
[\[23b.6\]]chang 'thung ba dang ma la sogs pa len pa'i yul gyi tha snyad phan tshun 'gal ba dag gis rnam par dpyad pa na de dag thams cad kyang lhung bar gyur to/_/de ltar de dag 'gal bar gzigs nas rgyal po grags pas gsungs pa/_'dir bdag gis bcom ldan 'das 
[\[23b.7\]]dus kyi 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor gyi khang ba 'dir gzhug par bya zhing khyed rnams la 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dbang sbyin par bya'o/_/gzhan yang bdag gi bkas khyed rnams kyi rdo rje'i rigs dang lhan cig tu bza' ba dang btung ba dang bag ma blang ba'i 'brel 
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]pa dag kyang bya'o/_/ci ste khyed rnams bdag gi bka' mi byed pa de lta na bdag gi grong bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug por la gang dang gang du khyed cag mos pa der khyed rnams song shig_/rnam pa gzhan du na lo brgyad brgya 'das pa'i rjes la khyod kyi bu dang 
[\[24a.2\]]tsha bo la sogs pas kla klo'i chos dar bar byas nas sham+b+ha la la sogs pa'i yul chen po dgu bcu rtsa drug tu kla klo'i chos ston par byed par 'gyur ro/_/kla klo'i lha bi Shi mi l+la'i sngags kyis phyugs kyi ske gri gug gis bcad de de nas lha'i sngags kyis bsad pa'i phyugs de rnams 
[\[24a.3\]]kyi sha za bar byed cing rang gi las kyis shi ba rnams kyi sha za bar mi byed par 'gyur ro/_/chos de yang khyed la tshad ma ste/_mchod sbyin don du phyugs dag spros zhes pa'i gzhung gi tshig las so/_/kla klo'i chos dang rig byed kyi chos dag la yang khyad par med de srog gcod 
[\[24a.4\]]par byed pa'i phyir ro/_/de'i phyir khyed kyi rigs kyi bu dang tsha bo la sogs pa rnams kyis kla klo de dag gi gzi byin dang g.yul du bdud kyi lha 'jug pa yang mthong nas ma 'ongs pa'i dus lo brgyad brgya 'das pa na kla klor 'gyur ro/_/de dag kla klor gyur pa na grong bye ba phrag 
[\[24a.5\]]dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa'i rigs bzhi la sogs pa thams cad kyang kla klor 'gyur te skye bo chen po gang nas bgrod pa de ni lam mo zhes tshangs pa'i drang srong gis smras pa'i phyir ro/_/'dir kla klo'i chos dang rig byed kyi chos la yang lha dang pha mes kyi don du srog gcad par bya ba yin 
[\[24a.6\]]la rgyal rigs kyi chos la yang ngo /_/lha dang pha mes tshim byas nas/_/sha zos skyon gyi cha yod min/_/zhes pa dang /_de bzhin du/_gang zhig gdug la gdug spyod pa/_/de la skyon ni ma mthong ngo /_/zhes bram zes smras pa'i phyir ro/_/de ltar rig byed kyi 
[\[24a.7\]]chos tshad mar byas nas kla klo'i chos yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur te/_rgyu des na ma 'ongs pa na kla klo'i chos mi 'jug par bya ba'i phyir khyed rnams la bdag gis nges pa sbyin no/_/de bas na khyed rnams kyis bdag gi bka' bzhin bya'o/_/zhes so/_rgyal po grags pa'i 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]gsung chad pa dang bcas pa'i bka' de skad brjod pa 'dis mgor thog lhung ba bzhin gyur pa'i tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams kyis nyi ma'i shing rta la smras pa/_kye nyi ma'i shing rta khyod kyis mi'i dbang po grags pa la bdag cag ni rig byed las bshad pa rigs kyi chos dor nas rdo rje'i 
[\[24b.2\]]rigs kyi dbang bskur ba'i chos la mi 'jug ste de bas bdag cag khyod kyi bkas shI ta dang kha ba can gyi lho lang+ka'i gling gi byang 'phags pa'i yul du 'gro ba mchog go zhes zhu ba mdzod cig ces so/_/tshangs pa'i drang srong de dag gi tshig gis nyi ma'i shing rtas mi'i dbang po grags pa 
[\[24b.3\]]la/_kye rgyal po chen po mchog gi rgyal po dbang phyug dam pa skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis dang dpe byad brgyad cu'i yan lag yongs su rdzogs pa dpal shAkya'i rigs kyi thig le mchog gi thugs rje can rang rang gi rigs kyi chos la zhugs pa rnams la thugs rje mdzod 
[\[24b.4\]]cig_/ci ste bdag cag gis gdon mi za bar khyod kyi bka' bzhin bya ba yin pa de lta na bdag cag rdo rje'i rigs kyi dbang bskur la 'jug par mi bgyi ste khyod kyi bkas chu bo shI ta'i lho kha ba can dang lang+ka'i gling gi bar 'phags pa'i yul du 'gro ba mchog go zhes zhus so/_/de nas nyi 
[\[24b.5\]]ma'i shing rta'i tshig gis rgyal po grags pas khyed rnams sham+b+ha la'i yul nas myur du byung la song shig_/gang gi chu bo shI ta'i byang phyogs grong bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa'i sems can thams cad srog gcod pa la sogs pa mi dge ba'i lam yongs su btang nas bcom 
[\[24b.6\]]ldan 'das dus kyi 'khor lo'i byin rlabs kyis yang dag pa'i ye shes kyi lam thob par 'gyur ro zhes smras so/_/rgyal po grags pa'i bka' des ka lA pa'i grong nas nges par byung ba'i tshangs pa'i drang srong de dag thams cad nyi ma bcu pa la nags tshal gyi nang du rab tu zhugs so/_/
[\[24b.7\]]nags tshal gyi nang du rab tu zhugs pa de dag thams cad rgyal po grags pas mngon par shes pa lnga'i stobs kyis mkhyen te/_ji ltar tshangs pa'i drang srong 'di dag 'phags pa'i yul du song na grong bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa'i sems can thams cad mi bzad pa'i 
[\[25a\]]
[\[25a.1\]]sems dang ldan par 'gyur te/_'dir rgyu gang zhig rdo rje'i theg par gsungs pa yang dag pa'i ye shes kyi lam ma yin pa'i rgyu des drang srong thar pa don du gnyer ba 'di dag thams cad dpal rgyal po grags pa'i 'jigs pas rang gi gnas yongs su btang ste rang gi 
[\[25a.2\]]gnyen bshes bzung nas 'phags pa'i yul du song ngo zhes rgyal rigs la sogs pa'i skye bo rnams sems par byed cing skal ba ngan par 'gyur te zab pa dang rgya che ba'i chos kyi snod la sems legs pa dang mi ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/de ltar sems can thams cad kyi rang gi sems kyi bsam 
[\[25a.3\]]pa mkhyen nas mi'i dbang po grags pas khyab 'jug dang tshangs pa dang drag po'i rigs thams cad rmongs par byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so/_/ting nge 'dzin de dang lha'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs kyis drang srong de dag thams cad nags tshal de nyid du 
[\[25a.4\]]rmongs par gyur pa dang nags tshal de na gnas pa'i ri khrod pa la sogs pa rnams kyis drang srong thams cad bcings nas slar yang dkyil 'khor gyi khang pa nyid du khyer 'ongs te mi'i dbang po grags pa'i zhabs kyi drung du dor ba dang rab tu sad de mi'i dbang po grags pa dang /_dkyil 
[\[25a.5\]]'khor gyi khang pa de nyid dang ma la ya'i skyed mos tshal mthong bar gyur to/_/de mthong ba na ya mtshan thob nas e ma ho/_'di ni ngo mtshar che ste bdag cag rab tu ma sad par nags tshal chen po nas dkyil 'khor gyi khang pa chen por sus khyer te 'ongs zhes pa'i tshig 'di smras so/_/tshangs 
[\[25a.6\]]pa'i drang srong rnams kyi tshig de lta bu 'di rgyal po grags pa'i blon po sprul pa'i sku rgya mtsho'i blo gros kyis thos nas smras pa/_kye nyi ma'i shing rta la sogs pa tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams ya mtshan par ma byed cig_/rgyal po grags pa 'di ni phyogs gcig pa ma yin te khyed rnams 
[\[25a.7\]]rjes su gzung ba'i don du sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis byung ba'i byang chub sems dpa' chen po zhig go/_/de'i phyir 'di'i zhabs la skyabs su song la rgyud kyi rgyal po dang po'i sangs rgyas la 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dngos grub sgrub pa'i lam dbang bskur 
[\[25b\]]
[\[25b.1\]]bar gsol ba thob cig ces so/_/de nas rgya mtsho'i blo gros kyi tshig de dang sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis nyi ma'i shing rta la sogs pa tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams rab tu sad de tshig 'di smras pa/_legs so legs so/_/rgya mtsho'i blo gros khyod kyi tshig gang gis bdag 
[\[25b.2\]]cag gi sems rab tu sad par gyur to/_/de'i phyir nga ni dkon mchog gsum la skyabs su song ste sems can mtha' dag gis skye ba 'di nyid la yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thob par bya ba'i slad du rgyud kyi rgyal po dus kyi 'khor lo la 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das 
[\[25b.3\]]pa'i dngos grub sgrub pa'i lam dbang bskur bar gsol ba gdab par bgyi'o zhes so/_/de skad smras nas drang srong de rnams kyis tshangs pa'i rigs kyi rgyal po nyi ma'i shing rta la bos te/_kye nyi ma'i shing rta khyod ni rig byed la sogs pa'i bstan bcos kyi glegs bam gcig 
[\[25b.4\]]pu 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i bstan bcos thams cad kyi gzhal bya 'dzin pa'i thugs can no/_/des na bdag cag gi gsol ba 'debs pa'i tshig gis rgyal po grags pa la gsol ba gdab pa byos shig_/bdag cag kyang maN+Dal sngon du 'gro ba can gyis 
[\[25b.5\]]skyabs su song ste thams cad kyis gsol ba gdab par bgyi'o/_/de nas tshangs pa'i drang srong gi tshig des nyi ma'i shing rtas rin chen gser gyi rang bzhin gyi me tog rnams kyis maN+Dal byas nas mi'i dbang po grags pa'i zhabs kyi drung du rin po che'i me tog gi snyim pa gtor te/_
[\[25b.6\]]pus mo gnyis sa la btsugs shing lag pa gnyis spyi bor son pas tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams dang thabs cig tu grags pa'i zhabs la phyag 'tshal nas/_pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs shing thal mo sbyar ba dpral bar bkod de mi'i dbang po grags pa la gsol ba 
[\[25b.7\]]btab pa/_bcom ldan 'das gang du mtshams med pa lnga byed pa po rnams kyang skye ba 'di nyid la sangs rgyas nyid thob par lung ston zhing bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa'i mchog tu mi 'gyur ba'i bde bas yang dag par bsdus pa'i phyag rgya chen po 'thob par lung ston pa rgyud kyi rgyal 
[\[26a\]]
[\[26a.1\]]po dang po'i sangs rgyas bstan du gsol/_de bzhin gshegs pas rgyal po zla ba bzang po la bstan pa'i dang po'i sangs rgyas stong phrag bcu gnyis pa gang yin pa de nyid gzhung nyung ngus dang po'i sangs rgyas rgyud kyi rgyal po bsdus pa 'di yang dag par bsdu ba 
[\[26a.2\]]mdzad nas tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams la ston pas bstan du gsol zhes so/_/de nas nyi ma'i shing rtas gsol ba btab pa gsan nas tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams kyi mos pa'i sems kyi dbang gi phyir de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs kyis bsdus pa yang dag 
[\[26a.3\]]par sdud par mdzad pa po nyid kyis phreng ba 'dzin pa'i tshigs su bcad pa thams cad mkhyen pa ston pa po la sogs pa'i yang dag par sdud par byed pa dag gis rgyud kyi rgyal po ston par mdzad do/_/sgra bzang po smra ba de dag rnams kyi sgra bzang po la zhen pa spang ba'i slad du don 
[\[26a.4\]]la rton pa nyid la brten te tshigs su bcad pa kha cig tu zur chag gi sgra dang /_tshigs su bcad pa kha cig tu gcod mtshams nyams pa dang /_kha cig tu rnam dbye med pa'i tshig dang /_kha cig tu dbyangs dang gsal byed phyis pa dang /_tshigs bcad kha cig tu ring po la thung ngu 
[\[26a.5\]]dang thung ngu la yang ring po dang /_kha cig tu lnga pa'i don la bdun pa dang /_bzhi pa'i don la drug pa dang /_kha cig tu gzhan gyi tshig can gyi byings la bdag nyid kyi tshig can dang /_bdag nyid kyi tshig can la gzhan gyi tshig can dang kha cig tu gcig gi tshig la mang po'i tshig 
[\[26a.6\]]dang /_mang po'i tshig la gcig gi tshig dang /_kha cig tu pho'i rtags la ma ning gi rtags dang ma ning gi rtags la pho'i rtags dang /_kha cig tu rkan las byung ba sha yig la so dang spyi bo las byung ba dang /_kha cig tu spyi bo las byung ba la so dang rkan las byung ba dang /_kha 
[\[26a.7\]]cig tu so las byung ba la rkan dang spyi bo las byung ba ste/_de lta bu gzhan yang rgyud ston pa po'i man ngag gi rjes su 'brang bar bya ba'o/_/de bzhin du rtsa ba'i rgyud las bcom ldan 'das kyis/_zla bzang sangs rgyas thams cad dang /_/bla ma rnams kyi slob ma yis/_/
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]chung ma bu mo bu sdug dang /_/rtag tu 'dod pa'i dngos po 'bul/_/dri ni sa la 'byung ba ste/_/chu la gzugs dang me la ro/_/rlung reg 'gyur ba med la sgra/_/nam mkha' che la chos kyi dbyings/_/dri dang spos sogs mar me dang /_/bza' dang btung sogs gos 
[\[26b.2\]]rnams kyis/_/rtag tu phyag rgya mchod byas te/_/dam pa'i bu yis bla ma'i dbul/_/zhes gsungs te de lta bu la sogs pa'i sgra zur chag gzhan yang rnal 'byor pas lung bklags pa las rtogs par bya'o/_/de bzhin du 'grel bshad la yang sgra bzang po'i mngon pa'i nga 
[\[26b.3\]]rgyal nyams par bya ba'i slad du bdag gis don la rton pa nyid la brten te bri bar bya ste/_rnam pa gang dang gang gis rigs dang rig pa dang sgra bzang po'i mngon pa'i nga rgyal zad par 'gyur ba'i rnam pa de dang des don la rton pa nyid la brten te yul gyi skad gzhan dang sgra'i bstan 
[\[26b.4\]]bcos kyi skad gzhan gyis sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams thar pa'i don du chos ston to/_/bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po dus kyi 'khor lo'i 'grel bshad rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rjes su 'jug pa stong phrag bcu gnyis pa dri ma med pa'i 'od las ston pa po dang gsol ba 
[\[26b.5\]]'debs pa po dang rtsa ba'i rgyud dang bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi 'grel pa'i mdor bsdus te gsum pa'o//_//da ni 'jam dpal gyi sprul pa mi'i dbang po grags pa dang nyi ma'i shing rta ston pa po dang gsol ba 'debs pa po la sogs pa'i 'brel pa la sogs pas 
[\[26b.6\]]bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po rnam par rgyas par bya ste 'dir dpal ldan dus kyi 'khor lo chen po'i dkyil 'khor gyi khang pa'i shar gyi sgo dang nye ba'i nor bu rin po che'i khyams chen por rin po che'i seng ge'i khri chen po la bzhugs pa/_lha dang lha ma yin dang klu'i sprul pa'i sku rgyal 
[\[26b.7\]]po'i rigs chen po dgu bcu rtsa drug tu skyes pa rin po che'i cod pan chen po bcings pa grong bye ba bye ba'i bdag po rnams kyis zhabs kyi pad+ma la phyag byas pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab kyi sku nyi ma'i shing rtas gsol ba btab pa mi'i dbang po grags 
[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]pas nyi ma'i shing rta la 'di gsungs so/_/nyi ma'i shing rta khyod tshangs pa'i drang srong gi rigs la sogs pa/_sems can thams cad kyis yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi lam thob par bya ba'i slad du rgyud kyi rgyal po mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas 
[\[27a.2\]]dam pa'i dngos po nyan par 'dod cing yang dag par brtson pa gang yin pa de ni legs so legs so/_/khyod kyi 'di legs pa de bas na rgyud kyi rgyal po mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas dam pa'i dngos po 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dngos grub sgrub par byed 
[\[27a.3\]]pa dus kyi 'khor lo'i rdo rje'i rnal 'byor khyod kyis dris pa de thams cad bdag gis bstan par bya'o/_/sdud par byed pa po rgyal po grags pa bdag gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi stobs kyis yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang zla ba bzang po ston pa po dang 
[\[27a.4\]]gsol ba 'debs pa por 'brel pas phreng ba 'dzin pa'i sdeb sbyor gyis yang dag par bsdus te rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las byung ba sngon du lung bstan pa'i bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po dam pa'i dngos po shes rab dang thabs kyi bdag nyid kyi rnal 'byor khyod kyis kyang rtse gcig pa'i 
[\[27a.5\]]yid kyis nyon cig ces so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gyi sprul pa'i sku mi'i dbang po grags pas mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las de bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan pa ston pa po la sogs pa yang dag par sdud pa'i tshigs su bcad pa dang po gsungs pa/_
[\[27a.6\]]thams cad mkhyen pa ye shes sku dang nyin mor byed pa'i sku ste pad+ma'i 'dab ma rgyas pa'i spyan/_/sangs rgyas seng ge'i khri la bzhugs pa lha mchog rnams kyis btud la rgyal po zla ba bzang po yis/_/mgo bos rab tu phyag 'tshal lag pa'i pad+ma sbyar ba yan lag mchog 
[\[27a.7\]]la bzhag nas zhus pa ni/_/rnal 'byor dpal ldan dus 'khor ka phreng ldan pa'i a 'dus la ste mi rnams dgrol ba'i don du'o/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po 'dir ston pa po la sogs pa rnams bsdu ba'i don du tshigs su bcad pa dang po 'di bcom ldan 'das kyis yang dag par 
[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]gsungs so/_/'dir ston pa po la sogs pa rnams ni ston pa po dang /_gnas dang /_'khor chen po dang /_gsol ba 'debs pa po dang /_chos bstan pa dang /_dgos pa dang dgos pa'i dgos pa'o/_/'di rnams las re zhig ston pa po bsdu ba ni/_/thams 
[\[27b.2\]]cad mkhyen pa ye shes sku dang nyin mor byed pa'i sku ste pad+ma'i 'dab ma rgyas pa'i spyan/_/sangs rgyas zhes pa'i yi ge nyi shu rtsa gsum po 'dis bcom ldan 'das kyis ston pa po bsdu ba mdzad do/_/seng ge'i khri la bzhugs zhes pa'i yi ge lnga po 'dis gnas bsdu ba mdzad do/_/
[\[27b.3\]]pa lha mchog rnams kyis btud la zhes pa'i yi ge bdun po 'dis 'khor chen po bsdu ba mdzad do/_/rgyal po zla ba bzang po yis/_mgo bos rab tu phyag 'tshal lag pa'i pad+ma sbyar ba yan lag mchog la bzhag nas zhus pa ni/_/zhes pa'i yi ge nyi shu rtsa brgyad po 'dis 
[\[27b.4\]]gsol ba 'debs pa po bsdu ba mdzad do/_/rnal 'byor dpal ldan dus 'khor ka phreng ldan pa'i a 'dus la ste zhes pa'i yi ge bcu bzhi po 'dis chos bstan pa bsdu ba mdzad do/_/mi rnams dgrol ba'i don du'o/_/zhes pa yi ge bdun po 'dis dgos pa dang dgos pa'i 
[\[27b.5\]]dgos pa bsdu ba mdzad de/_de ltar na yi ge brgyad cu rtsa bzhi po 'di rnams kyis rim pa ji lta ba bzhin ston pa po dang gnas dang 'khor chen po dang gsol ba 'debs pa po dang chos ston pa dang dgos pa dang dgos pa'i dgos pa rnams de bzhin gshegs pas lung 
[\[27b.6\]]bstan pa'i bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gyis rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las phyung ste bsdu ba mdzad do/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po thams cad du 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan pa de bzhin 
[\[27b.7\]]gshegs pas gsungs pa ma yin nam/_ji ltar rgyud kyi rgyal po 'di thams cad mkhyen pa zhes pa la sogs pas 'di skad bdag gis la sogs pa dang bral bar sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang dag par bstan pa yin/_'di skad bdag gis thos pa la sogs 
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]pas bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan pa med pa'i phyir rgyud kyi rgyal po 'di sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa ma yin no zhes ming dang tshig la rton pa la brten pa 'ga' zhig gi tshig 'dir 'byung bar 'gyur te/_/de'i phyir brjod pa 'dir 
[\[28a.2\]]ma 'ongs pa'i dus na byis pa'i skye bo ming dang tshig la rton pa la brten pa dag gis rgyud kyi rgyal po thams cad du 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan pa de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs 
[\[28a.3\]]par gang brjod pa de ni ma yin te/_gang gi phyir chos ston pa po don la rton pa la brten pa nyid kyi phyir dang /_sdud par byed pa po rnams kyang yul sna tshogs kyi skad gzhan gyis yang dag par sdud par byed pa'i phyir ro/_/'dir gal te 'di skad bdag gis thos 
[\[28a.4\]]pa zhes pa la sogs pa legs par sbyar ba'i tshig mgrin pa dang rkan la sogs pa'i 'bad rtsol gyis bskyed pa nyi tshe bas de bzhin gshegs pa chos ston par byed pa de lta na chos kyi phung po brgyad khri bzhi stong dus du mar yang de bzhin gshegs pas bstan pa dang /_sdud pa pos kyang 
[\[28a.5\]]bri bar mi nus te nyi tshe ba legs par sbyar ba'i tshig gcig pu yin pa'i phyir ro/_/'dir sprul pa'i sku mtha' yas pa med pa'i phyir mtha' yas pa mtha' yas pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams rnams su mtha' yas pa mtha' yas pa'i sems can rnams kyi sgra dag gis 
[\[28a.6\]]cig car chos ston pa yang med par 'gyur ro/_/chos kyi phung po brgyad khri bzhi stong ston par byed pa med pa'i phyir thams cad mkhyen pa ma yin par 'gyur te de ltar yang ma yin no/_/'dir sangs rgyas tshad med pa dang chos tshad med pa dang dge 'dun tshad med par de 
[\[28a.7\]]bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa 'di'i phyir ston pa po thams cad mkhyen pa gcig ma yin no/_/thams cad mkhyen pa'i skad sems can thams cad kyi skad kyis don ston par byed pa gcig ma yin no/_/bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan pa gcig ma yin no/_/
[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsol ba 'debs pa po nyan thos kyi dge 'dun gcig ma yin gyi/_'dir sems can rnams kyi mos pa sna tshogs kyi dbang gis thams cad mkhyen pa du ma dang thams cad mkhyen pa'i skad du ma dang bzhugs pa'i gnas du ma dang gsol ba 'debs pa po 
[\[28b.2\]]du ma dang chos bstan pa du ma yin no/_/de'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pas 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pa mgrin pa dang rkan la sogs pa'i 'bad rtsol gyis bskyed pa lha'i skad gcig pus rgyud thams cad bstan pa ma yin no/_/'dir dang por re zhig nyan thos 
[\[28b.3\]]kyi tshul sde snod gsum la sogs pa ma ga d+hA'i skad kyis chos ston pa ni 'di lta ste/_it+ya pi so b+ha ga bA samyak+saM bud+d+ho bid+yA tsa ra Na saM pan+naHsu ga to lo ka bi du a nut+ta ro zhes pa la sogs pas chos bstan to/_/de bzhin du chu bo shI ta'i byang tsam pa ka'i yul du grong bye ba'i skad 
[\[28b.4\]]kyis chos ston pa ni 'di lta ste/_a kar Sh+Tu khu Sh+Nu ka ga ga Na ku/_dzi Na ma ka bi dzi ri Ta ku/_du du ru ba ka ces pa la sogs pas chos bstan to/_/de bzhin du ru ka ma'i byang su ram+ma'i yul du grong bye ba'i skad kyis chos ston pa ni 'di lta ste/_a ka ma ya sad/_ba la da da ta ta 
[\[28b.5\]]bi ra Ta/_mi ni ka a ku Ta ba ra dad/_dzi gi ti ba ra data/_ces pa la sogs pas chos bstan to/_/de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa yul du ma'i yul gyi skad kyis theg pa gsum ston par byed pa'i phyir 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pa legs par sbyar ba'i skad gcig 
[\[28b.6\]]pus ma yin no/_/des na sdud par byed pa po rnams kyis legs par sbyar ba'i skad kyis bris pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po rnams la yang kha cig tu ni 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pas bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan te/_kha cig tu ni ma yin no/_/dang por re 
[\[28b.7\]]zhig dpal 'dus pa stong phrag nyi shu rtsa lnga pa dang sgyu 'phrul dra ba stong phrag bcu drug pa zhes pa la sogs pa rgyud kyi rgyal po du mar ni 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pas bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan to/_/la lar ni ma yin te dang por re zhig mchog gi dang po'i 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]sangs rgyas stong phrag bcu gnyis pa dang /_rnal 'byor rjes su rig pa stong phrag sum cu rtsa drug pa dang /_mngon par brjod pa 'bum pa chen po las te/_de bzhin du rgyud kyi rgyal po du ma rnams la yang ngo /_/rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po rnams la ji lta ba de bzhin du 
[\[29a.2\]]de nas phyung ba'i bsdus pa'i rgyud rnams la yang kha cig tu ni 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pas bstan la kha cig tu ni ma yin no/_/'dir dpal 'dus par bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[29a.3\]]thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje btsun mo'i b+ha ga rnams la bzhugs te zhes gnas nges par bstan pa gsungs so/_/de bzhin du sgyu 'phrul dra ba las 'di skad bdag gis thos pa zhes pa la sogs pas bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan to/_/de bzhin du rtsa ba'i 
[\[29a.4\]]rgyud dang bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po gzhan rnams la yang bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan to/_/'dir mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las bcom ldan 'das kyis/_thams cad mkhyen pa ye shes sku/_/nyi ma'i sku gang mi zad pa/_/dpal ldan pad 'dab rgyas pa'i 
[\[29a.5\]]spyan/_/sangs rgyas seng ge'i khrir bzhugs pa/_/sku gsung thugs dang chags pa yis/_/sku gsung thugs kyi dkyil 'khor la/_/sku gsung thugs dang chags pa'i bdag_/rdo rje sems dpa' lhag pa'i lha/_/mi phyed rdo rje'i rnal 'byor 'di/_/dus kyi 'khor lo 
[\[29a.6\]]mi 'gyur bde/_/sangs rgyas thog ma tha ma med/_/dang po'i sangs rgyas rigs med pa/_/kun nas rdo rje skal bzang nyid/_/kun nas sna tshogs sdom pa ste/_/bden don rnam pa bcu gnyis dang /_/de nyid rnam pa bcu drug 'dzin/_/zhes pa la sogs pas mchog 
[\[29a.7\]]gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan pa gsungs so/_/de bzhin du rnal 'byor rjes su rig pa stong phrag sum cu rtsa drug pa las bcom ldan 'das kyis/_rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i pad+mar gnas/_/lhag pa'i lha ni gcig po 'di/_/lhan skyes 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]dga' ba'i gzugs kyis ni/_/srid gsum bdag nyid dag la gnas/_/zhes pa la sogs pas rnal 'byor rjes su rig pa las bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan pa gsungs so/_/de bzhin du mngon par brjod pa 'bum pa las bcom ldan 'das kyis/_pho nya 
[\[29b.2\]]kun gyi gsang ba la/_/sems dpa' kun gyi bdag nyid gnas/_/sems dpa' pho nya kun rang bzhin/_/rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba che/_/zhes pa la sogs pas bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bstan pa gsungs te de yang bla ma'i man ngag las rtogs par bya'o/_
[\[29b.3\]]de bzhin du gsol ba 'debs pa po ni dpal 'dus pa las/_de nas phyag na rdo rje de bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pas byin gyis brlabs par gyur pas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po rgyud thams cad kyi bla na med pa sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dpal 
[\[29b.4\]]'dus pa sangs rgyas thams cad mngon par brjod pa bcom ldan 'das kyis bstan du gsol zhes pa de lta bu la sogs pas phyag na rdo rjes gsol ba btab bo/_/de bzhin du sgyu 'phrul dra ba la phyag na rdo rje gsol ba 'debs pa po ste mtshan yang dag par brjod pa las

[\[30b.1\]]'phags pa'i yul 'dir sgra smra ba po mu stegs pa mkhas pa'i mngon pa'i nga rgyal dang ldan pa rnams mthong nas/_ji ltar bram ze dang khyab 'jug pa dang zhi ba pa la sogs pa rnams kyi 'dod pa'i lha tshangs pa dang khyab 'jug dang drag po las sogs pa rnams legs par sbyar ba smra

[\[31a.1\]]bde ba go bar byed pa'i phyir ro/_/de bzhin du e yig dang wa~M yig dag gis rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i stong pa nyid dang chos thams cad la dmigs pa med pa'i snying rje dbyer med pa'i byang chub kyi sems kyi dngos po go bar byed pa'i phyir/_e wa~M gi

[\[31b.1\]]bsgyur bar 'gyur te phyi ma'i a yig sgra gcan dang rnam par shes pa dang lhan cig wa nyid do/_/rjes su nga ro zla ba dang khu ba dang yang dag par sbyar te de'i phyir wa~M yig go/_/de bzhin du dang po'i a yig nam mkha'i khams dang gsang ba'i pad+ma ni bdun pa'i mtha' can te/_a

[\[34a.1\]]yang dag par bstan to/_/'dir 'ga' zhig la thams cad mkhyen pa gang yin pa de nyid sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das ma yin nam/_ming gnyis kyis dngos po gcig go bar byed pa'i phyir ro/_/sangs rgyas zhes pa zlos pa'i tshig 'di bcom ldan

[\[34a.7\]]'joms pa/_/zhes so de'i phyir thams cad mkhyen pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das te sangs rgyas zhes pa zlos pa'i tshig tu mi 'gyur ro/_/thams cad mkhyen pa gang yin pa de nyid ye shes sku ma yin nam/_bcom ldan 'das kyis ye shes sku zhes zlos pa ci'i don du

[\[44a.1\]]rnams sgrib pa dang bral ba nyid dang ro mnyam pa nyid dang gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa nyid la stong pa zhes brjod do/_/thams cad dngos po med pa ni ma yin te rnal 'byor pas rang rig pa nyid kyi phyir ro/_/de nyid ni rgyal ba rnams kyis gzhom du med

[\[69b.1\]]pa rnams kyi gza'i 'bras bu skyes rabs la sogs pa rnams don med do/_/'dir grub pa'i mtha' la gza' rnams kyi bgrod pa bud med dang byis pa rnams kyis bsgrub par bya ba yin na byis pa'i skye bo la sogs pa de dag rnams kyis gza' myur du shes par 'gyur ro/_/des na phrag

[\[71a\]]

[\[73a.1\]]bar gyur pa na rkang pa brgyad pa snga ma bdun dang bcas pa rnams dor te gzhan ma spyad pa'i rkang pa drug po rnams tshogs gcig tu byas nas chu tshod nyi shu rtsa bzhi sbyin pa'am dor bar 'gyur ro/_/dgu pa la rkang pa lnga po rnams kyi chu tshod nyi shu rtsa gnyis so/_/bcu pa la

[\[73b\]]

[\[78a\]]
[\[78a.1\]]las grub pa'i mtha' rnams rnam par nyams zhes pa'i tshig gis bsdus te/_rgyud las gsal por ma mdzad do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'dir mu stegs pa rnams kyis bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po mthong ste/_nyi ma'i longs spyod rnam par dag pa mthong nas/_bdag

[\[78a.7\]]bsres pa zhes pa ni byed pa la ltos pa yin gyi grub pa'i mtha' la ltos pa ma yin no/_/mig dang nyi ma zhes pa brgya nyi shu rtsa gnyis bsres pa'o/_/mig gis rnam 'gyur zhes pa ni phyogs kyi phung po'i don du gnyis kyis bsgyur ba'o/_/zla ba'i tshigs la gcig bsres te 
[\[78b\]]
[\[78b.1\]]zhes pa ni nya'i longs spyod kyi don du phyogs gcig bsres pa dang gnam stong gi longs spyod kyi don du phyogs gnyis bsres pa phyogs kyi phung por 'gyur ro/_/mkha' dus chu yis rab phye zhes pa ni/_sgra gcan gyi phyogs kyi longs spyod kyi don du phyogs kyi 'khor lo phyogs bzhi brgya drug cus bgos 
[\[78b.2\]]te mkha' dus chu des rab tu phye ba'i lhag ma'i phyogs gang yin pa de dag thub pa lag pas bsgyur bar 'gyur ro/_/skar ma'i longs spyod kyi don du mkha' sogs cha yis skar ma'o zhes pa ni stong pa la sogs pa bzhi brgya drug cu'i chas thob pa skar mar 'gyur ro/_/slar yang lhag ma ni lhag ma drug cus 
[\[78b.3\]]bsgyur ba gang yin pa las cha de nyid kyis thob pa chu tshod du 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du drug cus bsgyur ba las cha de nyid kyis chu srang du 'gyur ro/_/drug gis bsgyur ba las cha de nyid kyis dbugs su 'gyur ro/_/thob pas dman pa'i 'khor lo gdong 'dir mkha' dus chu yis thob pa'i skar 
[\[78b.4\]]ma la sogs pa gang yin pa de dag gis 'khor lo skar ma nyi shu rtsa bdun gyi bdag nyid la dman par byas pa'i 'khor lo ni gdong du 'gyur te tha skar la sogs pas gdong gi skar ma'i longs spyod du 'gyur ro/_/mun can ni sgra gcan te 'di la mun pa yod pa'i phyir mun can no/_/mun can de'i

[\[78b.6\]]gi tshogs gsungs pa zla ba zhes pa byed pa'i zla ba'o/_/nyin zhag gi phung po'i don du sum cus bsgyur bas de dag nyin zhag tu 'gyur ro/_/nyin zhag de dag da ltar ba'i zla ba'i nyin zhag so so dang bcas pa gnas gsum du 'gyur ro/_/'og gi phung po las ri mkha' ris thob pa zhes pa

[\[79a\]]
[\[79a.1\]]'gyur ro/_/de'i phyir gsal ba'i nyin zhag gi tshogs su 'gyur ro/_/da ni bkra shis gsungs te ri dus gcig gis bsres pa zhes pa ni byed pa la ltos pa yin gyi grub pa'i mtha' la ltos pa ma yin no/_/brgya drug cu rtsa bdun gyis bsres pa ni ri dus gcig gis

[\[79a.4\]]pa'i cho gas so/_/'dir gza' thams cad kyi longs spyod kyi gnas rnam pa lngar 'gyur te/_khyim gyi gnas dang /_skar ma'i gnas dang /_chu tshod kyi gnas dang /_chu srang gi gnas dang /_dbugs kyi gnas rnams 'og nas 'og tu 'gyur ro/_/dbugs kyi gnas su dbugs

[\[79a.7\]]spyod gcig la skye ba'i rkang pa gzung ba'i don du skar ma dgu sbyang bar bya'o/_/phyed bcas zhes pa ni chu tshod sum cu chu tshod kyi gnas nas sbyang bar bya'o/_/tha skar la sogs pa'i skar ma'i longs spyod la gal te bu lon lhag na 'khor lo bsres la rnam par sbyang zhes pa ni/_'dir 
[\[79b\]]
[\[79b.1\]]skar ma'i longs spyod tha skar la sogs pa la gdon mi za bar bu lon sbyang bar bya ste gal te bu lon gyi phung po lhag pa dang bcas shing longs spyod kyi phung po dman par gyur na/_de'i tshe 'khor lo bsres la rnam par sbyang ngo /_/'khor lo ni skar ma nyi shu rtsa bdun gyi tshogs te de dag

[\[79b.5\]]pas so/_/grangs bzhin du rig par bya'o/_/'dir khyim rgyu ba'i rkang pa ni de nyid ces pa skye ba nas khyim dang po la nyi shu rtsa lnga'o/_/gnyis pa la [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad do/_/gsum pa la ri zhes pa bdun te/_khyim snga ma gsum la'o/_de nas 'khor lo phyed kyi phyed gzhan la ni khyim bzhi pa la

[\[80a\]]
[\[80a.1\]]rtsa lnga'i chas thob pa ni snga ma phyed la gzung byar 'dod de/_rkang pa'i dbang gis zhes pa ni gza' phyed phyi mar ma bgrod kyi bar du'o/_/de'i 'og tu de bzhin phyi ma phyed la dor byar 'gyur zhes pa ni longs spyod de nyid phyed phyi ma la dor byar 'gyur ro/_/zos pa tshogs gcig byas

[\[80a.3\]]chu tshod la'o zhes pa la gza' rnams kyi bgrod pa ni rim pa min pa dang rim pa ste/_de'i dbang gis sbyin pa dang dor ba ni/_rim pa min pa'i dbang gis sbyin pa dang rim pa'i dbang gis dor ba'o/_/chu tshod la'o zhes pa ni chu tshod kyi phung po dang gza'i longs spyod skar ma'i phung po la'o zhes bya ba ni bkra

[\[80b\]]
[\[80b.1\]]pas nor du 'gyur ro/_/nges par zhes pa gdon mi za bar ro/_/rim pa min pas bu lon du 'gyur ro/_/gza' mthon po'i longs spyod dag pa'i lhag ma las 'khor lo phyed yongs su dor te sbyangs pa'i lhag ma ni 'dir rkang pa'o/_/gza' mthon po'i longs spyod rnam par dag pa'i lhag

[\[80b.4\]]pas sbyin pa dang rim pa min pas dor ba ste phyed snga ma la zos pa'i tshogs dang phyed phyi ma la ma zos pa'i tshogs ni snga ma bzhin no/_/sa skyes la ni rkang pa rgyal ba sogs te zhes pa la rgyal ba ni nyi shu rtsa bzhi'o/_/de dag kyang dang po la gnas pa ste ma nu'i gnas gnas bcu bzhi la

[\[81a.1\]]dman zhes pa ni brgyas bsgyur ba'i nyin zhag gi tshogs de nyid bdun stong brgya nyi shu rtsa gsum gyis dman pa'o/_/ri nas bug pa ri dang klu yis bgo ste zhes pa la ri nas ni bdun nas te bug pa ri dang klu zhes pa ni brgyad stong bdun brgya dgu bcu rtsa bdun te rang gi rkang pa'i

[\[81b\]]
[\[81b.1\]]gnas lnga pa la bdun no/_/khyim gyi gnas drug pa la bcu ste bcu ri me ni dal ba'i las la rkang pa'o/_/myur ba'i las te rgyu skar gyi las la mi bdag la sogs ni bcu drug la sogs pa ste gnas bcu bzhi la rkang pa bcu bzhir 'gyur ro/_/de dag kyang 'chad par 'gyur ba las

[\[81b.3\]]mkha' ro dang mig gis dman zhes pa ni byed pa la ltos pa yin gyi grub pa'i mtha' la ltos pa ma yin no/_/so me chu gter dag gis bgo zhes pa ni bzhi stong sum brgya sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis bgo ba'o/_/lhag ma ni bgos pa'i lhag ma ste skar mas bsnun pa ni nyi shu rtsa bdun gyis bsgyur 
[\[81b.4\]]ba las dkyil 'khor gyi chas skar mar 'gyur ro/_/slar yang sngar brjod pa'i rim pas bla ma'i longs spyod la yang chu tshod la sogs par 'gyur ro/_/rgyu skar nyi ma sbyang ste zhes pa ni phur bu'i longs spyod 'di las skar ma bcu gnyis sbyang bar bya ste bya ba lhag ma ni mig dmar bzhin no/_/
[\[81b.5\]]'phrog byed dgu dang rtse mo can ni dal ba'i las la rkang pa'o zhes pa ni sngar bzhin du khyim gyi gnas drug la ste 'phrog byed ces pa bcu gcig go/_/de nas dgu'o/_/de nas rtse mo can zhes pa gsum ste de bzhin du phyed phyi ma las nga lugs las bzlog pas so/_/slar yang myur ba'i

[\[82a\]]
[\[82a.1\]]rkang pa dag pa'i don du'o/_/lhag ma skar mas bsgyur ba las kyang skar ma'i longs spyod slar yang chu tshod kyi longs spyod mig dmar bzhin te de bzhin du chu srang la sogs pa'i longs spyod ni myur ba'i las la ste bas ngas kyi yin no/_/pa sangs kyi dal ba'i las la yang

[\[86b.1\]]bdun pa la nyin mtshan mnyam par 'gyur ro/_/de phyir gnyis brgyad dum bu dag la nges par glang la gnas dang sdig pa la gnas de bzhin no zhes pa ni 'dir ji ltar sngar brjod pa la lhun po'i lho dang byang gi sa'i dum bur lug gi nyi ma dang /_srang gi nyi ma la nyin mtshan mnyam par 
[\[86b.2\]]'gyur ba de bzhin du cung zad bden bral gyi zur du gnas pa dang dbang ldan gyi zur phyed kyi char gnas pa la yang nges pa/_zhes pa gdon mi za bar nyi ma glang la gnas pa dang /_sdig pa la gnas pa la de bzhin du dpyid phyed dang ston phyed nyin mtshan mnyam par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du bug 
[\[86b.3\]]pa zhes bya dgu pa dang me grangs te gsum pa'i sa'i dum bur bden bral gyi zur gyi phyed gzhan la gnas pa dang dbang ldan gyi zur phyed la gnas pa la nyin mtshan mnyam par 'gyur te/_/nyi ma 'khrig pa la gnas pa dang /_gzhu la gnas pa la dpyid phyed dang ston phyed du 'gyur ro/_/
[\[86b.4\]]phyogs kyi tshad bcu pa dang bzhi bdag la ni lhun po'i shar gyi sa'i dum bu dang nub kyi sa'i dum bur nyin mtshan mnyam par 'gyur te nyi ma kaR+ka Ta la gnas pa dag dang chu srin la gnas pa la dpyid phyed dang ston phyed du 'gyur ro/_/lnga pa la gnas pa dang bcu gcig pa la yang mdun gyi sa'i dum 
[\[86b.5\]]bur nyin mtshan mnyam par 'gyur te dpyid phyed dang ston phyed du 'gyur ro/_/seng ge bum par rab tu zhugs par gyur pa na ste nyi ma lnga pa rlung gi zur gyi phyed la gnas pa dang bcu gcig pa me'i zur gyi phyed la gnas pa la nyin mtshan mnyam par 'gyur te dpyid phyed dang ston phyed du 'gyur ro/_/
[\[86b.6\]]bcu gnyis ro yi sar ni zhes pa bcu gnyis pa me'i zur gyi phyed gzhan la gnas pa dang ro rlung gi zur gyi phyed gzhan la gnas pa la ste sa zhes pa lung gi tshig go/_nyi ma nya la gnas pa dang bu mo la gnas pa la nyin mtshan mnyam pa gnyis su 'gyur te dpyid phyed dang ston phyed du 'gyur ro/_/
[\[86b.7\]]de ltar sa bcu gnyis su khyim bcu gnyis kyi dbang gis nyi ma 'char ba dang nub pa'i rnam par dbye bas zla ba bcu gnyis dang dus drug tu 'gyur ro/_/da ni nyi ma'i khyim 'khor ba'i dbang gis sa dum bu bcu gnyis su byang du bgrod pa bcu gnyis dang lhor bgrod pa bcu gnyis gsungs pa 
[\[87a\]]
[\[87a.1\]]'dir ni g.yon bgrod du 'gyur zhes pa ste/_'dir ni zhes pa lhun po'i lho'i pa'i dum bur ro/_/ci'i phyir zhe na lho 'dzam bu'i gling chung ngur de bzhin gshegs pas chos bstan pa'i dbang gis so/_/de'i phyir lho'i sa'i dum bur g.yon bgrod ces pa byang du bgrod pa ste nyi ma chu srin 
[\[87a.2\]]du gnas pa la'o/_/de bzhin du bum par nyi mas sa'i dum bu gnyis pa la'o/_/nya la dum bu gsum par ro/_/mi rnams kyi ni bgrod pa dag kyang lug gi nyi mas bzhi par te nub kyi sa'i dum bu ni bzhi pa'o/_/'byung ba zhes bya lnga par nyi ma glang la gnas par gyur pas so/_/'khrig par 
[\[87a.3\]]nyi ma gnas pas sa drug pa la byang du bgrod par 'gyur ro/_/bdun pa lhun po'i byang du yang nyi ma kaR+ka Ta la gnas pas byang du bgrod par 'gyur ro/_/seng ge la gnas pas sa'i dum bu brgyad par 'gyur ro/_/bu mo la nyi ma gnas pas bug pa ste sa'i dum bu dgu par ro/_/srang gi 
[\[87a.4\]]nyi mas sa'i dum bu bcu par 'gyur ro/_/sdig par nyi ma yis drag po'i dum bu bcu gcig pa la'o/_/gzhu la gnas pas dum bu bcu gnyis par 'gyur te mi rnams bgrod pa dag kyang zhes pa srid pa ste khyim bcu gnyis kyi dbye bas rtsibs bcu gnyis kyis la gnas pa'i 'jig rten rnams 
[\[87a.5\]]kyi byang du bgrod pa'o/_/de bzhin lhor bgrod du 'gyur/_de ltar brjod pa'i rim pas lhor bgrod pa ste nyi ma'i bgrod pa'i dbang gis kaR+ka Ta sogs khyim rnams dag la 'gyur ro/_/lhun po'i lho 'dzam bu'i gling chung ngu'i sa'i dum bu 'dir kaR+ka Ta ra nyi ma gnas pas lhor bgrod par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[87a.6\]]de bzhin du g.yas skor gyis te gnyis par seng ge'i nyi mas so/_/gsum par bu mo'i nyi mas so/_/bzhi par srang gi nyi mas te lhun po'i nub tu lhor bgrod par 'gyur ro/_/lnga pa la sdig pa'i nyi mas so/_/drug pa la gzhu'i nyi mas so/_/bdun pa la chu srin gyi nyi mas te lhun po'i byang du'o/_/

[\[87b.2\]]rang gi dum bur kaR+ka Ta la sogs pa rang rang gi lhor bgrod pa'i 'pho ba'i nyin zhag la 'gyur te dum bu bcu gnyis po rnams la rim pa bzhin no/_/de nas g.yas phyogs su yang zad par 'gyur te mi yi bdag po stong phrag dag ni drag po'i grangs zhes pa ni/_lhor bgrod pa'i 'pho ba'i 
[\[87b.3\]]nyin zhag de nas g.yas su bgrod pa la byang du bgrod pa'i bar du stong phrag bcu gcig zad par 'gyur te/_nyi ma'i gdung bar byed pa ni steng du stong phrag bdun cu rtsa lngar rig par bya'o/_/stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa gcig mda' ni ste lnga brgya dang bral bar gyur pa dbus nas 'go

[\[87b.7\]]byang bgrod la gnas pa'i nyi ma lho bgrod kyi dang po'i nyin zhag gi bar du'o/_/mdang rnams tshes kyis bsgyur ba zhes pa la mda' rnams zhes pa lnga'o/_/tshes zhes pa [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]lnga ste [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]lnga po de dag gis bsgyur ba ni mda' rnams tshes kyis bsgyur ba zhes pa ste bdun cu rtsa lngar 'gyur ro/_/dpag

[\[88a.1\]]tshad kyi grangs stong phrag bdun cu rtsa lnga po de phyogs kyi bgrod pa dag la nyin mor byed pas 'gro bar byed de gangs can nas me'i phar dang /_me nas gangs can gyi bar stong phrag bdun cu rtsa lnga ni lho bgrod la sogs pa'i phyogs kyi cha'o/_/phyogs kyi cha de zla ba drug gis rim pa

[\[88b.1\]]dpyid la sogs pa'i dus rnams dang /_nag pa la sogs pa'i zla ba rnams dang /_de bzhin du phyogs rnams thams cad rab tu grags so/_/de'i phyir lug gi khyim de la nyi ma gnas pas dum bu bcu gnyis su dus kyi tshogs la sogs pa thams cad byed pa ji lta ba de bzhin du brjod par

[\[89a.1\]]'gro'o/_/de na gnas pa ni zur phyed gzhan du 'gro'o/_/zur phyed gzhan na gnas pa ni shar lus 'phags por 'gro'o/_/shar lus 'phags po na gnas pa ni me'i zur gyi phyed kyi dum bur 'gro'o/_/phyed snga ma na gnas pa ni zur phyed gzhan du 'gro'o/_/zur phyed

[\[89b.3\]]gung dang phyi 'bred do/_/thun mtshams ni bzhi ste mtshan phyed kyi thun mtshams dang /_snga dro'i thun mtshams dang /_nying gung gi thun mtshams dang /_phyi 'bred kyi thun mtshams so/_/thun tshod ni brgyad de nyin mo la bzhi dang mtshan mo la bzhi'o/_/nyin mo dang mtshan mo dus kun tu ni yang dag

[\[90a.1\]]rkang pa dang bcas zhes pa ni dpag tshad nyis brgya lnga bcu lhag pa'i stong phrag drug ste khyim la gnas pa'i nyin mor byed pa 'di nam mkha' la 'gro ba'o/_/de'i phyir 'khor ba'i shugs 'di ni khyim gyi yin gyi gzhi la gnas pa'i nyi ma yi ni ma yin no/_/'dir khyim gyi 'khor lo ni

[\[90a.6\]]pa'i don to/_/'go la dag la g.yas dang ni g.yas min dag tu 'grib dang 'phel ba lnga ste zhes pa ni 'go la'i phyed de las g.yas dang g.yas min dag tu ste g.yas su zla ba gsum gyis mtshan mo chu tshod lnga 'phel lo/_/g.yas min du zla ba gsum gyis nyin mo chu tshod lnga 'phel

[\[90b.1\]]ba'am 'grib pa ni dbugs gnyis dang chu srang gsum mo/_/tshad 'di ni kai lA sha'i dum bu la yin gyi 'phags pa'i yul la sogs pa la ma yin no/_/'phags pa'i yul du yang nyin mtshan so so la chu srang gnyis 'phel ba'am 'grib par shes par bya ste zla ba gsum
